### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Lost_on_Campus_Evaluating_Embodied_Spatial_Reasoning_of_Vision-Language_Models_in_the_Wild|Lost on Campus: Evaluating Embodied Spatial Reasoning of Vision-Language Models in the Wild]]

![[assets/2611.33145_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33145](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33145)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33145
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Lost_on_Campus_Evaluating_Embodied_Spatial_Reasoning_of_Vision-Language_Models_in_the_Wild|Lost on Campus: Evaluating Embodied Spatial Reasoning of Vision-Language Models in the Wild]]
- **作者**: Zehan Zheng, Yanyuan Chen, Deming Li, Yutao Tang, Jianwen Xie, Alan Yuille, Jieneng Chen, Cheng Peng
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.3（加权：具身智能 1.2，大模型 0.1）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Agent, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《Lost on Campus: Evaluating Embodied Spatial Reasoning of Vision-Language Models in the Wild》归入 具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Ego3D-Bench, GOAT-Bench, NavBench, OpenBench, STI-Bench, Sage-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Embodied Spatial Reasoning (ESR) is central to deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in real-world embodied tasks, yet remains inadequately evaluated. Existing benchmarks primarily assess spatial reasoning in static images or synthetic indoor environments, where semantic shortcuts often suffice, making it difficult to faithfully evaluate spatial reasoning capabilities in the wild. In light of this, we present Lost on Campus, a benchmark for evaluating ESR in large-scale real-world outdoor 3D environments reconstructed by 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our benchmark introduces a unified reasoning-action evaluation framework that seamlessly integrates diagnostic QA for isolated reasoning with closed-loop interactive navigation for active reasoning under multimodal instructions. To enable fine-grained diagnosis, we evaluate six capability aspects of ESR: action grounding, spatial foresight, metric awareness, goal-directed planning, ego-allocentric localization, and spatio-temporal consistency. Extensive experiments reveal that: (i) Compared to indoor settings, real-world outdoor environments impose substantially higher demands on spatial reasoning, where existing models exhibit poor performance; (ii) VLMs still struggle with fine-grained visual alignment, with failures in spatial foresight and ego-allocentric localization emerging as primary bottlenecks; (iii) Improving multimodal interaction capabilities and long-term spatial reasoning is crucial for advancing embodied intelligence. These findings highlight that faithful evaluation of ESR demands benchmarks tightly coupling perception, reasoning and action within realistic and continuous environments.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/PyRUA-Dex_Learning_Programmatic_Policies_for_Dexterous_Bimanual_Manipulation_with_Coding_Agents|PyRUA-Dex: Learning Programmatic Policies for Dexterous Bimanual Manipulation with Coding Agents]]

![[assets/2611.33144_figure.png|800]]

- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/PyRUA-Dex_Learning_Programmatic_Policies_for_Dexterous_Bimanual_Manipulation_with_Coding_Agents|PyRUA-Dex: Learning Programmatic Policies for Dexterous Bimanual Manipulation with Coding Agents]]
- **作者**: Feixiang Ruan, Guodong Lv, Kewei Zhang, Wenbo Huang, Yintianrun Zhang, Qiang Wang, Ruiyang Si, Shunyu Yang, Shulong Jiang, Weicheng Duan, Rui Ni, Jianxin Bi...
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 大模型, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.3（加权：具身智能 1.5，大模型 0.5，机器人 0.3）
- **关联关键词**: Agent, Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《PyRUA-Dex: Learning Programmatic Policies for Dexterous Bimanual Manipulation with Coding Agents》归入 具身智能、大模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、大模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Learning for dexterous manipulation is limited by the scarcity of high-quality demonstrations: retargeting from human video is imprecise, teleoperation gives no force feedback to the operator, and exoskeleton-based collection is hard to scale up. We present PyRUA-Dex, a framework that enables coding agents to develop dexterous manipulation through programmatic policy, without the need of demonstrations. The programmatic policy is developed in two stages: during exploration, multiple agents explore in parallel for task skills and ways to compose them, and a distilling agent condenses the successful attempts into a task library and one program. During refinement, this program is improved through randomized layouts for enhanced robustness and execution efficiency. In simulated environments, PyRUA-Dex achieves SOTA on Bench2Dex with a score of 72.9 and reaches 81.0% success on DexJoCo under full randomization, ahead of every trained policy on both benchmarks. In real world experiments on a bimanual platform with two 22-DoF hands, PyRUA-Dex learns to play piano, write calligraphy, and other tasks efficiently: learning a new piece from sheet music takes as little as 30 minutes. These results suggest that PyRUA-Dex is an effective framework for developing dexterous manipulation policies with coding agents in the absence of high quality dexterous manipulation demonstrations.

</details>

- **作者**: Xingjian Ran, Xiaoye Mo, Sihao Liu, Li Luo, Taiqiang Wu, Ji Zhao, Wenqi Shao, Hongyang Li, Jiaqi Wang, Bo Dai
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《ROAM-Bench: Evaluating Embodied Active Spatial Intelligence through Evidence Verification》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：E3VS-Bench, EQA, ESI-Bench, EXPRESS-Bench, EfficientEQA, EmbodiedBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Embodied spatial intelligence requires agents to gather information through exploration and interaction, then use it to reason and act. Existing benchmarks insufficiently distinguish correct outcomes from completion of these task requirements. We introduce ROAM-Bench to evaluate these abilities under robot motion and collision constraints. Independent checks use simulation state and episode records to assess outcomes and task requirements, with stronger checks of geometry, coverage, or placement. It comprises 250 manually validated tasks across 11 types in five groups and 27 indoor environments, covering object and room search, counting, geometric judgments, direction reasoning, and relocation. Human evaluators achieve 84% strict task success on 100 sampled tasks, with every correct outcome satisfying the required checks. Across seven frontier vision-language models, we identify two complementary failure modes: correct outcomes with unmet task requirements, and incorrect outcomes despite passing stronger checks. Oracle-input diagnostics show that more complete target observations and calibrated feedback improve final spatial judgments.

</details>

- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/EmbodiSafety_Benchmarking_Risk_Understanding_and_Response_in_Embodied_AI|EmbodiSafety: Benchmarking Risk Understanding and Response in Embodied AI]]
- **作者**: Erfei Cui, Shengjia Zhao, Yan Qin, Kaixuan Wang, Junming Chen, Ping Luo, Tianxing Chen
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《EmbodiSafety: Benchmarking Risk Understanding and Response in Embodied AI》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EmbodiedBench, HomeSafeBench, IS-Bench, SafeAgentBench, SafeMindBench, SafeVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Progress in embodied foundation models is largely measured by task success, while the physical safety of their decisions remains underexamined. Safety evaluations centered on rule following and binary judgments do not adequately assess whether embodied models understand environmental hazards and make safe decisions. We introduce EmbodiSafety, comprising 1,092 snapshot tasks and 540 trajectory tasks across 844 visual scenarios to assess safety over short and long horizons. Six response modes cover direct execution, refusal, clarification, constrained execution, autonomous recovery, and human escalation. Distinguishing recovery from human intervention makes the boundary of safe autonomy an explicit evaluation target. Our protocol assesses mode correctness, risk identification, and actionable handling, with complete decision accuracy requiring all applicable checks to pass. Claude-Opus-5.5 achieves the highest complete decision accuracy among reference models, reaching 56.1% on snapshots and 61.5% on trajectories. We further develop a data construction pipeline and curate 40k examples for safety alignment. Supervised training on this corpus raises the complete decision accuracy of Qwen3.5-2B and Qwen3.5-4B.

</details>

![[assets/2611.33013_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33013](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33013)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33013
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/RFW-W_Scaling_Physical_Asset_Universes_for_Generative_Embodied_Simulation|RFW-W: Scaling Physical Asset Universes for Generative Embodied Simulation]]
- **作者**: Jiawei Weng, Rui Zhang, Saining Zhang, Changjian Pan, Yuanbo Lyu, Jiarui Guo, Yongzhi Li, Guolin Liu, Jialu Liu, Jiankun Wang, Yi Yang, Peishuo Li...
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.3（加权：具身智能 1.2，大模型 0.1）
- **关联关键词**: Agent, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《RFW-W: Scaling Physical Asset Universes for Generative Embodied Simulation》归入 具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DexGraspNet, GAPartNet, PartNet, RoboFlywheel-World, ShapeNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Scaling embodied simulation requires diverse objects with executable physical representations. We present RFW-W, a physical asset universe connecting rigid, articulated, and deformable asset sources to task-conditioned scene composition. Its reported inventories comprise 1,010,386 asset records: 524,611 rigid assets in 1,212 categories (RFW-R), 402,410 articulated assets in 531 categories (RFW-A), and 83,365 jointly verified deformables in 485 categories (RFW-S). These taxonomies contribute 2,228 dataset--category entries. RFW-R couples category-guided generation with collision geometry, composed physical properties, and interaction annotations; RFW-A uses reusable agent-authored procedural generators with explicit part and joint structure; RFW-S uses structure-aware simulation representations, calibration, and independent testing. A common interface preserves physical requirements specific to each asset type for retrieval and scene assembly. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate that RFW-R provides broader asset coverage and stronger scene-fulfillment capabilities than existing datasets. Across five RoboTwin tasks, using 100 demonstrations per task for each asset source, policies trained on RFW-R achieve macro success rates of 71.2% in clean settings and 41.8% under randomization, exceeding the strongest baseline by 15.8 and 10.6 percentage points, respectively. RFW-W provides a scalable and unified asset foundation for generating physically grounded, task-conditioned embodied worlds.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Embodied_AI_Fails_the_Mirror_Test_How_a_Simple_Mirror_Breaks_Robot_Policies|Embodied AI Fails the Mirror Test: How a Simple Mirror Breaks Robot Policies]]

![[assets/2611.33010_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.33010](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33010)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.33010
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Embodied_AI_Fails_the_Mirror_Test_How_a_Simple_Mirror_Breaks_Robot_Policies|Embodied AI Fails the Mirror Test: How a Simple Mirror Breaks Robot Policies]]
- **作者**: Xuanchen Liu, Hejun Zhang, Boxuan Dong, Ying Wu, Heshuai Li, Wen Gao, Yonghong Tian, Weihao Yu
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 4.6（加权：具身智能 3.3，机器人 1.3）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《Embodied AI Fails the Mirror Test: How a Simple Mirror Breaks Robot Policies》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：HallusionBench, IllusionVQA, MIRA-Bench, MirrorBench, MirrorQA, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Can a simple mirror expose the limitations of today's robotic manipulation policies? Inspired by the mirror self-recognition test in animal cognition, we investigate whether robot policies can reliably manipulate physical objects in scenes containing their reflections. We introduce RoboDojo-Mirror, a benchmark built on RoboDojo that preserves task objectives, success criteria, and policy checkpoints while comparing a mirror-free baseline with single-mirror, dual-mirror, and mirrored-tabletop conditions. Across five policies spanning Vision-Language-Action models, World Action Models, and agentic policies, every mirror condition reduces aggregate success rates: averaged over the three conditions, the drop ranges from 10.8 to 56.7 percentage points across policies, and it reaches 62.5 points on the mirrored tabletop. Because a mirror is also a physical obstacle, we compare it against geometrically matched non-reflective panels; 50--88\% of mirror-specific failures occur without recorded robot--panel contact, so direct collisions alone do not explain the degradation. Trajectory and contact analyses instead reveal recurring behaviors consistent with phantom targeting of reflected objects and spatial mislocalization of real ones. Targeted diagnostics further characterize grasping near reflections, policy-dependent pushing progress, and physical-target interaction under main-camera occlusion. Real-robot experiments with $π_{0.5}$ and FastWAM also show lower overall success rates under the upright-mirror and mirrored-tabletop conditions. These findings reveal a shared vulnerability to reflective environments across diverse robot policies and motivate reflection-aware evaluation of embodied intelligence. Code is available at https://github.com/iPPLab/robodojo-mirror.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/VidForce_Predicting_Force-Aware_Articulation_by_Observing_Humans|VidForce: Predicting Force-Aware Articulation by Observing Humans]]

![[assets/2611.32967_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32967](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32967)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32967
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/VidForce_Predicting_Force-Aware_Articulation_by_Observing_Humans|VidForce: Predicting Force-Aware Articulation by Observing Humans]]
- **作者**: Rafay Mohiuddin, Jiaqi Chen, Marc Pollefeys, Hermann Blum
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.2（加权：具身智能 0.6，大模型 0.1，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Agent, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《VidForce: Predicting Force-Aware Articulation by Observing Humans》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

People can anticipate how an object moves and the effort an interaction may require by watching others. How can embodied agents acquire this understanding from video? Force measurements provide direct sensory supervision, but require specialized equipment and remain limited in quantity. We introduce VidForce, which estimates an interaction trajectory together with a force profile from a third-person RGB video; 2D contact clicks and textual object-action prompts are optional. Our approach makes limited instrumented measurements useful for interpreting human interactions through learned physical prototypes as a prior. The prototypes share learned force levels and temporal profiles across interactions of comparable effort, with visual and semantic cues determining their contribution to each observation. Geometric and vision-language features adapt this prior to the observed interaction, while a joint conditional flow refines an initial tracked trajectory together with the force profile. Paired human and instrumented-gripper recordings provide measured force references for supervising human video, and a separately trained torque model demonstrates that the formulation extends to other sensory quantities. We evaluate trajectory recovery and force estimation through baseline comparisons and controlled ablations on held-out interactions from the Hoi! dataset. On this evaluation subset, VidForce achieves 6.6 cm ATE, peak-force MAE and RMSE of 4.81 N and 5.92 N, respectively, and force-profile RMSE of 5.52 N. On real-world videos, VidForce infers motion and force reliably and consistently despite the variability in human execution. We further deploy VidForce on a Spot robot, where trajectories and force profiles estimated from human demonstrations drive real-world articulated-object manipulation, and report success rates across repeated trials. Project page: https://rafaymhddn.github.io/vidforce

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/DynaMimic_A_Physics-Informed_World_Model_for_Humanoid_Motion_Tracking|DynaMimic: A Physics-Informed World Model for Humanoid Motion Tracking]]

![[assets/2611.32944_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32944](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32944)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32944
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/DynaMimic_A_Physics-Informed_World_Model_for_Humanoid_Motion_Tracking|DynaMimic: A Physics-Informed World Model for Humanoid Motion Tracking]]
- **作者**: Chaoyue Xing, Linyi Huang, Yiting He, Yixuan Yin, Siqi Hou, Yi Zhang, Liyuan Pan, Miaomiao Liu, Daqi Liu
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 世界模型, 强化学习
- **相关性评分**: 3.6（加权：具身智能 1.5，强化学习 0.2，世界模型 0.8，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: RL, WorldModel, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《DynaMimic: A Physics-Informed World Model for Humanoid Motion Tracking》归入 具身智能、机器人、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：NeuralSim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Whole-body motion tracking enables humanoid robots to reproduce diverse skills from human demonstrations. Existing model-free reinforcement learning methods either distill motion specialists into a single policy at the cost of performance or train a generalist policy directly using cluster-scale computation. SuperMimic reduces this computational cost through a differentiable world model that implicitly learns the physics shared across motions by predicting the next state from the current state and action. However, this implicit formulation may not fully capture the underlying physical processes. Errors around changes of support can accumulate over imagined rollouts used to train the tracking policy. To address this challenge, we introduce DynaMimic, a differentiable world model that explicitly incorporates shared physics into state prediction. Specifically, its network predicts a contact wrench at each candidate support link and a residual generalized force. DynaMimic then uses these predictions to compute the next state through closed-form Lagrangian forward dynamics. Across 60 full LAFAN1 motions, DynaMimic reduces mean per-joint position error (MPJPE) by 18.6% relative to SuperMimic. For terrain tracking, we give both methods the same local height maps. Relative to terrain-conditioned SuperMimic, DynaMimic reduces MPJPE by 14.9% on A3 Parkour (66.15 to 56.28~mm) and by 10.8% on GRAIL terrain clips (141.88 to 126.57~mm). On a real A3 humanoid, the learned policies can perform diverse skills, such as floor recovery, cartwheel and a box vault.

</details>

![[assets/2611.32900_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32900](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32900)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32900
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Motion_Conditioned_World_Modeling_for_Embodied_Visual_Tracking|Motion Conditioned World Modeling for Embodied Visual Tracking]]
- **作者**: Jiahao Zhang, Xuan Yao, Junyu Gao, Changsheng Xu
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 世界模型, 机器人

- **关联关键词**: Robotics, EmbodiedAI, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Motion Conditioned World Modeling for Embodied Visual Tracking》归入 具身智能、世界模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、世界模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EVT-Bench, Real-World, TrackVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models support embodied decision-making by predicting future environment states. In embodied visual tracking, uncertain target motion and robot-induced viewpoint changes jointly affect visual observations, making future-state prediction challenging. We propose Motion-Conditioned World Modeling, which incorporates predicted target-motion distributions and robot ego-motion into latent state transitions. A Geometry--Progress module jointly models geometric paths and single-step travel distances through shared motion modes and path-conditioned progress distributions. This formulation captures uncertainty in route selection and motion progress while preserving their dependence. Action-induced coordinate transformations express predicted paths in the next robot frame, providing geometrically grounded conditions for future visual-state prediction. Future visual feature reconstruction supervises the motion predictor through the conditioned transition branch, linking explicit motion representations to subsequent observations. The model is jointly optimized with motion likelihood, feature reconstruction, and expert-action supervision, enabling the tracking policy to combine visual context with predictive motion cues.Experiments on three embodied tracking benchmarks demonstrate improved tracking performance and cross-environment generalization with a lightweight $0.5$B backbone.

</details>

- **作者**: Yanzhe Chen, Zhijun Cao, Ruihe An, Chang Gong, Mike Zheng Shou
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《MRBench: Benchmarking Physical Necessity Reasoning in Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MRBench, RoCoBench, VIKI-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recent advances in multi-robot manipulation have shown how robots can collaborate to accomplish complex tasks. However, successful coordination does not reveal which robots are actually needed. The same task may require different teams as robot capabilities change, resources become unavailable, or objects replace robotic support. We argue that reliable multi-robot manipulation requires physical necessity reasoning: understanding what a task demands, which resources can fulfill those demands, and which are truly necessary. We introduce MRBench, a benchmark for physical necessity reasoning in heterogeneous multi-robot manipulation. It comprises eight tasks with distinct concurrency and reach constraints, each evaluated under five configurations that change the required resources while preserving the task goal. Our evaluation reveals that existing coordination agents perform well with reference teams but struggle when robot embodiments change or passive objects can replace robotic support. To address this gap, we propose MRScissors, which separates task requirements from measured physical capabilities and identifies minimal feasible teams through constrained search. Across two model backbones, MRScissors achieves 85.4% and 91.2% team-selection accuracy, outperforming the strongest baselines by 10.4 and 9.5 points, respectively. Together, these findings expose a critical gap between coordinating robots and understanding their physical necessity.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Embodied_Intelligence_for_Spectrum_Map_Construction_in_Low-Altitude_Wireless_Networks|Embodied Intelligence for Spectrum Map Construction in Low-Altitude Wireless Networks]]

![[assets/2611.32799_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32799](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32799)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32799
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Embodied_Intelligence_for_Spectrum_Map_Construction_in_Low-Altitude_Wireless_Networks|Embodied Intelligence for Spectrum Map Construction in Low-Altitude Wireless Networks]]
- **作者**: Hongtao Liang, Yiyao Wan, Jiahuan Ji, Lingda Meng, Fuhui Zhou, Qihui Wu
- **cs 子类**: cs.NI
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Embodied Intelligence for Spectrum Map Construction in Low-Altitude Wireless Networks》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Networking and Internet Architecture 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DRL, ECDNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Accurate spectrum map construction by unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is crucial for intelligent resource management in low-altitude wireless networks (LAWNs). Although various construction approaches have been investigated, their performance often degrades under extremely sparse sampling because purely data-driven models may not capture the underlying propagation physics. To address these challenges, an embodied intelligence framework is proposed, which synergistically integrates closed-loop perception-cognition-execution (PCE) coupling and embodied exploration to achieve precise and efficient spectrum construction. Based on this framework, a corresponding embodied cognition and decision network (ECDNet) with three tightly coupled modules is designed. Specifically, by leveraging distance-aware convolutions, the semantic bridge, and adaptive feature alignment mechanisms, ECDNet establishes the bidirectional information flow between construction and decision-making. Simulation results show that ECDNet achieves a root mean square error (RMSE) of 2.787 dBm at 4.00% sampling under the non-revisiting spiral protocol. Furthermore, a practical UAV-based spectrum construction platform is developed and field-deployed to provide prototype-level evidence that the sensing, cognition, and action modules execute as a closed loop on size, weight, and power constrained UAV edge devices.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Supply Geometry, Learn Execution: Screw Attention for Zero-Shot Policy Transfer in Robot Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We present the Screw Attention Policy, a geometry-centric manipulation framework that supplies task geometry explicitly and learns only execution. The policy operates on object poses, relative SE(3) transforms, and joint screw axes provided by perception and robot models. The policy requires only 30K learned parameters. Trained entirely in simulation on a single robot arm, the policy generalises to new scene layouts, transfers zero-shot across robot embodiments, and deploys directly on a real robot, without adaptation or fine-tuning. It achieves 96% success on LIBERO, 85.8% under layout changes, 80.2% across five unseen robot arms, and 21 of 24 trials on a real robot. These results suggest that much of what manipulation policies learn at scale is geometric structure that can instead be supplied.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Ctrl-World, PointWorld, Real-to-Sim, Real2Sim, WorldGym。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Video world models for robots are increasingly conditioned on a simulated or rendered proxy of the scene, on the assumption that the video will show what the proxy shows. Testing this assumption requires a proxy whose state is explicit and can be perturbed. We present SimPWM (Simulator-as-Proxy World Model): given a calibrated camera, it represents, from the first frame alone, the robot by its 3D model and the objects the gripper may touch as 3D points; a rule-based simulator with no trained parameters moves them under the actions, and a video model renders the result. SimPWM returns the realistic video together with the simulated state. We then freeze the simulated objects while the arm moves as recorded and check whether the video still shows the object lifted. On DROID episodes from unseen setups, the answer differs for the arm and for the objects. With each of three base video models, the arm follows the proxy (robot IoU 0.75-0.78, against at most 0.69 for end-to-end models); rendering the robot alone already gives this. What the simulated objects add is the object state: against a robot-only proxy, they cut missed pick-ups from 54% to 38%, and the video follows them only with some base models: on rigid objects, freezing them lowers the lift rate of MiniMax-H3, fine-tuned on episodes whose simulation agrees with the real video, from 42% to 10%, but that of LTX-2.3, trained on the same episodes, only from 57% to 49%, as if it inferred the grasp from the arm motion; more than half of the remaining lifts of MiniMax-H3 are of cloth and rope, which the simulator carries rigidly. With any base model, the simulated state records what the simulation did; under perturbed actions, a video that disagrees with it reveals that the video ignores the simulated state.

</details>

![[assets/2611.32722_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Hongcheng Zhang, Lei Sun, Songxuan Liu, Tengteng Pan, Yuqiang Zhai, Xianglong Xu, Jianya Guo, Xiao Song
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《AG-WAM: Attention-Guided World Action Model for Accurate and Efficient Robot Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recently developed World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and robot actions, providing predictive context for control. However, existing WAMs typically learn implicit attention allocation with limited explicit action-relevant attention guidance, leading to limited performance. To unleash strong capabilities of WAMs, we introduce explicit attention guidance as a unifying principle and propose our Attention-Guided WAM (AG-WAM), an end-to-end framework with three plug-and-play attention guidance modules that directly reformulate internal attention paradigm in WAMs: the Content-Adaptive Predictive Attention module, the Guidance-Point Visual Attention module, and the State-Conditioned Text Attention module. Without any external plugin module adopted, our end-to-end framework with the unifying principle of explicit attention guidance boosts the accuracy and efficiency of WAMs. Without using any private embodied training data, our AG-WAM establishes new state-of-the-art performance on multiple public robotic manipulation benchmarks including LIBERO-X, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin-2.0, significantly outperforming other methods, even those using massive private embodied training data by a notable margin, meanwhile notably improving training and inference efficiency.

</details>

![[assets/2611.32705_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Dan Zlotnikov, Nadav Alali, Ohad Ben-Shahar
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Knocked Off Course: Tracing Uncommanded Motion from Perception to Action in Embodied VLAs》归入 具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CameraBench, EVT-Bench, EgoDyn-Bench, OmTrackVLA, RobustVLA, TIC-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) enable simultaneous processing of egocentric video and textual target descriptions to produce action commands for embodied tracking platforms. However, these platforms rarely move exactly as commanded, in part due to unexpected environmental disturbances such as wind, unleveled terrain, and road bumps. Here we study how such models handle uncommanded egomotion. First, we explore performance across diverse disturbances by benchmarking three tracking VLAs on three datasets. We find that models differ in which environmental disturbances hurt them most, but all are sensitive to sudden target loss. Tracing egomotion from pixels to emitted actions, we identify two gaps. In representation, motion is reliably recovered through explicit relations between frames, but aggressive compression of past frames weakens small-motion readouts. In action, large rotations do reach the decision state, yet the decoded direction carries only a few percent of a mirrored rotation's effect on the turn. Targeting both gaps, we fine-tune one model, Uni-NaVid, on data amounting to just 1.7% of its original training set in a ladder of analysis-motivated variants. These gain 5-22 points after target-loss without degrading clean success; after a smooth loss, the best one improves success by 72% (relative), recovering 76% of its gap to clean. We also show that targeted fine-tuning improves recovery partly via a text-mediated action dependency, without new architecture, large-scale data collection or training from scratch.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《DiffGR00T: Execution-Aware Differentiable Training for Humanoid Whole-Body Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：HAF-VLA, WholeBodyVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong results in arm-centric manipulation, yet humanoid whole-body control (WBC) requires dynamic balance and tightly coupled full-body coordination. Existing hierarchical humanoid VLAs typically decouple motion generation from physical execution, leaving tracking and balance errors unable to directly optimize the VLA generator. We propose DiffGR00T, an end-to-end differentiable training framework that couples GR00T N1.7 with SONIC, a pretrained whole-body behavior foundation model. To enable execution-aware differentiable training, we replace the non-differentiable simulator with a learned, action-conditioned world model, allowing multi-step body-execution losses to backpropagate through the frozen whole-body controller and the flow-matching sampler directly into the VLA action head. On four HumanoidArena simulation tasks, DiffGR00T improves average task success by 12.5 percentage points over the supervised base VLA (59.2% to 71.7%), demonstrating that execution-aware gradients effectively align high-level motion generation with closed-loop whole-body dynamics.

</details>

![[assets/2611.32654_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32654](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32654)

- **作者**: Hongjia Liu, Dacheng Zhou, Minghao Fu, Chuanjun Guo, Tianze Wang, Qingtao Liu, He Wang, Li Yi

#### 研究背景与动机

《DexWAC: A Unified World-Actor-Critic Model for Dexterous Robot Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot demonstrations provide more than actions to imitate: they reveal how actions change the world and how those changes contribute to task success. Recent world-action models (WAMs) extend vision-language-action (VLA) models with the first signal by jointly predicting actions and future observations, yet this predictive ability serves only as an auxiliary loss for policy learning. Meanwhile, most policy improvement methods learn dynamics and values in separate models, increasing training overhead and limiting representation sharing between these complementary objectives. We introduce DexWAC, a unified world-actor-critic model that jointly learns an actor, an action-conditioned world model, and a bootstrapped critic in a single robot pretraining stage. Our key insight is that visual prediction encourages control-relevant features, while value supervision promotes task-relevant representations; together, they shape a representation suited to policy learning. Built on an image-editing backbone, DexWAC predicts, for each action chunk, the visual latent and proprioception at its endpoint together with its reward and continuation, so the world model alone efficiently yields complete imagined transitions without generating intermediate video frames. After adapting the world model and critic on real rollouts, we improve the pretrained actor through either PPO on imagined rollouts or critic-guided best-of-$N$ selection. DexWAC surpasses the large-scale pretrained VLA and WAM baselines in simulation and achieves 75% average success on two real-world tasks, compared with 45% for $π_{0.5}$. Its world model is more accurate and faster than video-based baselines, and ablations confirm that learning dynamics and value together improves the policy.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/BARE_Body-Aware_Reflexive_Evasion_for_Humanoid_Robots|BARE: Body-Aware Reflexive Evasion for Humanoid Robots]]

![[assets/2611.32642_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32642](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32642)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32642
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/BARE_Body-Aware_Reflexive_Evasion_for_Humanoid_Robots|BARE: Body-Aware Reflexive Evasion for Humanoid Robots]]
- **作者**: Zihao Li, Tong Chen, Hua Song, Zitong Ding, Zihao Xu, Ce Hao, Weibang Bai
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 强化学习
- **相关性评分**: 3.0（加权：具身智能 1.5，强化学习 0.2，机器人 1.3）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《BARE: Body-Aware Reflexive Evasion for Humanoid Robots》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：FootstepNet, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Dynamic obstacle avoidance is essential for safely deploying humanoid robots in real-world environments, where rapidly approaching objects require immediate evasive responses. Recent advances in reflexive evasion have focused primarily on quadrupeds, which typically rely on large-scale whole-body maneuvers. In contrast, humanoids can exploit their rich body articulation to evade through small, localized body adjustments. By moving threatened body regions without repositioning the entire body, these responses may require less execution time and less surrounding free space. However, local adjustments can alter mass distribution or support conditions, making it challenging to achieve rapid evasion while maintaining whole-body stability. To this end, we propose BARE (Body-Aware Reflexive Evasion), a humanoid reflexive evasion framework guided by human motion priors that enables rapid local evasion while maintaining whole-body stability. A risk-guided motion adapter is designed to select reference motions from a retargeted human evasive motion library based on the threatened body region and to adjust their temporal phases based on collision urgency, allowing the robot to reach evasive postures before potential contact. Conditioned on the adapted reference and link-level collision risk, a reflexive evasion policy learns through reinforcement learning to coordinate local body adjustments with whole-body stabilization. We validate BARE in simulation and real-world experiments, demonstrating its ability to perform targeted local evasion while maintaining whole-body stability.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Online reinforcement learning (RL) enables vision-language-action policies to improve through environmental interaction. However, in long-horizon manipulation, sparse success/failure feedback provides little information about where policies encounter bottlenecks. Moreover, repeated full-task rollouts also leave these bottlenecks weakly analyzed and insufficiently practiced. To this end, we introduce ReFocus, a progress-guided multi-round RL framework that alternates between Global and Local RL to identify task-specific bottlenecks and allocate additional practice to them. Specifically, during Global RL, we decompose each task into a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) of subtasks. Then a DAG-based Progress Reward is leveraged for both policy training and bottleneck diagnosis. Based on the identified bottlenecks, ReFocus selects Adaptive Practice Windows, defining task segments from an entry stage to a target stage. It allocates additional interaction to these segments during Local RL while updating the entire policy. After that, it evaluates the policy to perform the full task for the next round of RL, forming an iteration between Global and Local RL. Experiments on LIBERO-Long and RoboCasa365 show that ReFocus achieves success rates of 97.8% and 51.3%, respectively, compared with 90.6% and 39.3% for the $π_{0.5}$ baseline. Furthermore, we evaluate a real-world adaptation on three long-horizon tasks. On BreadBaker, ReFocus achieves 95% success, compared with 30% for the $π_{0.5}$. Project page: https://refocus-robotics.github.io.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Design,_Control,_and_Teleoperation_of_a_Dexterous_Robotic_Hand_with_Soft-Rigid_Hybrid_Fingers|Design, Control, and Teleoperation of a Dexterous Robotic Hand with Soft-Rigid Hybrid Fingers]]

![[assets/2611.32582_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32582](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32582)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32582
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Design,_Control,_and_Teleoperation_of_a_Dexterous_Robotic_Hand_with_Soft-Rigid_Hybrid_Fingers|Design, Control, and Teleoperation of a Dexterous Robotic Hand with Soft-Rigid Hybrid Fingers]]
- **作者**: Chao Liu, Wil J. Norton, Brandon Romero, Daniela Rus
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人

- **关联关键词**: Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《Design, Control, and Teleoperation of a Dexterous Robotic Hand with Soft-Rigid Hybrid Fingers》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Adding compliance to robot hands results in higher grasp quality and robust contact with the environment. Soft hands are inherently compliant, but are typically more complex to control and model than rigid equivalents. We extend prior work on soft-rigid hybrid robot fingers into a five-fingered dexterous gripper --- an integrated tendon-driven hand that is highly compliant and controllable. Structural compliance combined with a rigid skeleton, when realized in a five-fingered hand, allow us to use linear control to achieve soft-grasping generality. We evaluate the use of this hand in a variety of teleoperated manipulation tasks, demonstrating the effectiveness of the overall mechanical design and control strategy. Our contributions include: (1) Mechanical design of the a five-fingered Soft-Rigid Hybrid Hand, composed of 12 active degrees of freedom, demonstrating a high degree on generalization enabled by the soft-rigid architecture, with our gripper, able to complete 28 of the 33 (84.8\%) grasp postures in the human range. (2) Precise, high bandwidth (up to 1432deg/s extension speed, compared with 700deg/s for the equivalent human joint). (3) Position and task space controllers for the soft-rigid hybrid finger and whole hand. (4) A teleoperation framework for control of this hand, as well as demonstrations of its efficacy in a variety of grasping and manipulation tasks Further tests illustrate the hand's advantages in completing non-rigid manipulation tasks, such as picking up thin deformable objects (such as a dollar bill or folding cloth) as well as in interfacing with soft or delicate targets.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/MXI-MemICL_Towards_In-Context_Robot_Manipulation_through_Self-Retrieved_Interaction_Memory|MXI-MemICL: Towards In-Context Robot Manipulation through Self-Retrieved Interaction Memory]]

![[assets/2611.32564_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32564](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32564)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32564
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/MXI-MemICL_Towards_In-Context_Robot_Manipulation_through_Self-Retrieved_Interaction_Memory|MXI-MemICL: Towards In-Context Robot Manipulation through Self-Retrieved Interaction Memory]]
- **作者**: Jialong Peng, Shihao Zhou, Feng Yan, Wen Xiao, Haozheng Wang, Wenqi Ren, Wenyuan Sheng, Jian Qiao, Jiaxu Miao, Lin Ma, Chao Yang
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《MXI-MemICL: Towards In-Context Robot Manipulation through Self-Retrieved Interaction Memory》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：BehaviorVLA, OpenVLA, Real-World, StellaVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

In-context learning (ICL) offers robots a way to acquire new skills from a single demonstration without parameter updates. However, existing robotic ICL methods either rely on large-scale pretraining constrained by scarce, costly robot interaction data or require additional annotations yet struggle to generalize across tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose MXI-MemICL, a framework for memory-guided ICL in vision-language-action (VLA) models. By treating demonstrations as retrievable memory, MXI-MemICL enables in-context manipulation without additional large-scale pretraining or specialized annotations. Specifically, a visual-action causal encoder turns actions and their observed effects in demonstrations and completed interactions into reusable memory to guide execution. To retrieve experience suited to upcoming execution, an intention predictor forecasts the next stage's intent and uses it to query memory. The retrieved experience conditions the language backbone through experience-token prefixes and gated cross-attention, guiding action generation with a learned gate that regulates its influence. MXI-MemICL improves success over the baseline by +8.2% and +14.4% on RoboCasa365 and RoboTwin 2.0. Real-robot experiments show a +9.0% gain, with demonstrations adding +5.0% on seen and +16.7% on unseen tasks. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>

- **作者**: Yang Zhao, Xubo Yang

- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《EmbodiedStudio: A Task-Driven Simulation Generation Platform for Embodied Learning and Evaluation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：FactorSim, GenSim, RLBench, SceneEval。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Training and evaluating robots across diverse tasks requires a supply of simulation environments suited to those tasks. While recent scene and task generators offer a promising way to expand this supply, delivering a usable task environment still faces two practical gaps: generated scenes that look plausible in appearance often fail to support the intended task interactions or remain physically stable in simulation, and generated scoring programs frequently attempt task conditions without implementing them faithfully. We present EmbodiedStudio, a task-driven simulation generation platform that bridges these gaps by jointly authoring scene, task, robot-behavior, and rubric programs under closed-loop simulation feedback and independent review. Crucially, as the coding agent iteratively debugs scenes, behaviors, and stateful rubrics in simulation, this closed-loop process natively yields both forward construction experience for learning to build environments and physical execution experience for policy learning, all bound to frozen environment revisions for repeatable evaluation. Across scene generation, rubric evaluation, ablations, and learning studies, EmbodiedStudio substantially improves task readiness and rubric faithfulness while improving physical stability and retaining actionable supervision signals for layout authoring and policy adaptation.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Next-Manip_On-Demand_Agent-Policy_Collaboration_for_Robotic_Manipulation|Next-Manip: On-Demand Agent-Policy Collaboration for Robotic Manipulation]]

![[assets/2611.32533_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32533](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32533)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32533
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Next-Manip_On-Demand_Agent-Policy_Collaboration_for_Robotic_Manipulation|Next-Manip: On-Demand Agent-Policy Collaboration for Robotic Manipulation]]
- **作者**: Jiayi Liu, Ke Ye, Jiaming Zhou, Yufei Huang, Haotian Liang, Kun-Yu Lin, Junwei Liang
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Next-Manip: On-Demand Agent-Policy Collaboration for Robotic Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA, RMBench, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

General-purpose robotic manipulation requires task reasoning, failure recovery, and precise control, yet limited manipulation data make these abilities difficult to obtain from a single policy. Recent multimodal agents show promise in task reasoning and recovery but lack reliable fine-grained control and incur high inference cost, whereas learned policies provide efficient fine-grained execution but struggle beyond their learned skills. Although their complementary strengths suggest combining the two, doing so requires repeatedly deciding which executor should act as task demands change. Instead of relying on the agent for each decision, we present Next-Manip, an on-demand agent--policy collaboration framework that decouples executor allocation from costly agent inference. We propose an Agent Invocation Controller (AIC) that allocates control between the policy and the agent through a lightweight classifier of current execution demands. We further introduce Task Dynamics Context (TDC), which packages informative manipulation changes from in-context examples into compact task references to support both agent reasoning and executor allocation. Across two simulation benchmarks and real-world experiments, Next-Manip achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing agent inference costs.

</details>

![[assets/2611.32476_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32476](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32476)

- **作者**: Linjing You, Zeming Yang, Zhenhao Shen, Shengqiang Xu, Guanda Li, Wenxuan Li, Jiyu Guo, Jasper Lu, Kyle Xu, Shawn Xie, Junjie Zhu, Siyu Wu...
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《ViTacLab: Physically Consistent Visuo-Tactile Simulation, Benchmarking, and Representation Learning for Robotic Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World, ViTacBench, ViTacSim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Tactile sensing is essential for contact-rich robotic manipulation, but existing visuo-tactile simulators trade physical fidelity for scalability, and benchmarks largely focus on two-finger grippers. We present ViTacLab, a framework comprising ViTacSim, ViTacBench, and ViTacPolicy. ViTacSim uses a tri-modal tactile primitive: a global sparse contact-force lattice, a local dense force array, and tactile images. Anchored to PhysX contact-solver outputs, it corrects normal inconsistency through local stiffness alignment and reconstructs tangential forces under decoupled slip-stick regimes while preserving GPU parallelism. A joint projection preserves the sparse wrench and enforces local friction cones. ViTacBench spans parallel grippers, dexterous hands, and bimanual systems, with tasks ranging from vision-dominant to tactile-dominant regimes. ViTacPolicy aggregates variable numbers of heterogeneous tactile inputs using spatial-pose embeddings and a Transformer, and aligns force-array and tactile-image modalities through a two-stream VQ-VAE with a shared codebook. Controlled Hertzian-reference indentation and real-sensor sliding tests reduce normal and tangential force RMSE from 2.31 and 1.85 N to 0.42 and 0.53 N, respectively. Throughput reaches 2,482.9 tactile-pipeline FPS at 256 parallel environments. Simulation and real-world experiments show gains on several contact-rich tasks, while low absolute success on tactile-dominant tasks highlights the remaining difficulty of purely tactile control.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large language models can write competent robot navigation code, but not only does the research on the topic advance, a model may also, due to the volume of findings, fail to recall relevant findings and apply them to the task at hand. In this work, we test whether providing particular research papers as context improves the LLM-written navigation code for the Go2 quadruped, with the LLM (Claude Opus 5) and locomotion policy frozen. Specifically, we compare the navigation controllers the LLM writes when provided with and without particular papers across three settings: behavior trees, navigators edited from a shared base, and controllers written from scratch. When a maze's shortest route is blocked mid-episode, 2 of 8 controllers written with the context of an incremental-replanning paper complete all four trips under Isaac Sim physics, against 7 of 8 without it (two-sided Fisher's exact test, p = 0.041). This difference appears only under physics: in an idealized kinematic simulator, the controllers written with and without the paper are indistinguishable. We hypothesize that an LLM provided research papers as context tends to overweight both their content and their goals -- in this case, the efficiency of incremental replanning -- negatively affecting the fulfillment of the main task and leading to less robust code.

</details>

---

![[assets/2611.32304_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32304](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32304)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32304
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Sparse_Poison,_Strong_Hijack_Efficient_Trajectory-Level_Backdoors_in_Vision-Language-Action_Models_with_Only_1_%_Poisoning|Sparse Poison, Strong Hijack: Efficient Trajectory-Level Backdoors in Vision-Language-Action Models with Only 1\% Poisoning]]
- **作者**: Simin Huang, Jiawei Chen, Shaolong Zhu, Qiwei Liang, Zhaoxia Yin
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 2.3（加权：具身智能 1.8，大模型 0.1，机器人 0.4）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Sparse Poison, Strong Hijack: Efficient Trajectory-Level Backdoors in Vision-Language-Action Models with Only 1\% Poisoning》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：BackdoorVLA, BadVLA, DropVLA, OpenVLA, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models advance robotic manipulation, but their reliance on demonstrations exposes them to backdoor threats. Among these threats, trajectory-level targeted backdoors are particularly challenging and may pose greater safety risks than behavior-disrupting attacks: rather than merely causing task failures or local action deviations, they must sustain closed-loop redirection to complete an attacker-specified multi-step task. To reliably induce such behaviors, existing methods typically rely on high poisoning rates to obtain sufficient target-trajectory supervision. However, high poisoning rates may compromise clean-task performance, whereas low rates leave target-trajectory supervision scarce and backdoor learning vulnerable to interference from dominant clean-task updates. To resolve this trade-off at low poisoning rates, we propose a two-stage co-optimization framework guided by vision, language, and action. Stage I freezes the policy and uses differentiable rendering and multimodal supervision to optimize a 3D trigger's texture, translation, and scale, strengthening trigger-target associations from limited poisoned demonstrations. Building on the optimized trigger, Stage II applies source-selective dual-LoRA adaptation to decouple clean and backdoor updates and mitigate their interference, followed by learned column-wise calibration and analytic fusion into a unified policy. Experiments on OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, $π_0$, and $π_{0.5}$ across four LIBERO suites show consistent gains over all evaluated attack baselines. With only 1% poisoned data, our method achieves 86.4% target success and 95.2% clean-task success on OpenVLA-OFT in LIBERO-Spatial. Real-robot trials also demonstrate trajectory-level hijacking.

</details>

- **作者**: Yiming Zeng, Jiangyuxuan Dai, Yuheng Na, Tan Zhu Xuan, Zihan Lin, Xinfang Qi, Shijian Zhang, Chengqi Shi, Yihan Kuang, Zhide Zhong, Junjie He, Junfeng Li...
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《EgoLoAF: Learning Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from Egocentric Demonstrations with Hand-Object Affordances》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EgoVLA, WholeBodyVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Training humanoid vision-language-action (VLA) models relies heavily on costly real-robot teleoperation. Recent efforts convert egocentric human demonstrations into humanoid training data. However, most methods still require wearable motion trackers and leave important hand-object interaction cues implicit. Many also mix in real-robot teleoperation data for VLA post-training. These dependencies limit scalable data collection, while implicit interaction cues make precise contact harder to learn. To overcome these limitations, we propose EgoLoAF, the first paradigm to derive all training data for humanoid loco-manipulation directly from egocentric demonstrations without wearable motion trackers. We post-train the VLA on the converted data alone, without real-robot teleoperation demonstrations, and use explicit hand-object interaction cues to guide action generation. Specifically, to obtain humanoid action targets, we estimate torso motion from recorded camera poses and reconstruct both hand trajectories from video. From the same recording, we use each hand's current pose relative to the object to represent hand-object affordances. During post-training, we inject these affordances into the action expert through cross-attention while keeping the vision-language backbone frozen. Additionally, we release 128,400 egocentric demonstration episodes spanning 1,000 hours and 1,284 tasks, together with converted humanoid action trajectories and rendered robot-view observations. Across four real-world tasks, EgoLoAF nearly achieves teleoperation performance (73.1 vs. 74.4 in mean Task Progress Score), while achieving a mean 4.03 times improvement in data-collection efficiency.

</details>

- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《VLAs Execute, Code Recovers: 3D Skill Envelopes Bridge the Compositional Gap in Agentic Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Robotics 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Composing frozen Vision-Language-Action (VLA) skills under an agentic planner promises scalable long-horizon manipulation without long-horizon demonstrations, yet strong atomic skills routinely fail at seams even under a correct plan. Standard remedies reset the robot's pose or draft entry conditions in language, missing the physical cause we uncover via causal interventions: even after re-homing, a frozen VLA rigidly retraces its habitual trajectory and collides with displaced scene residue, whether or not it can see it. We propose the Skill Envelope, a policy-measured 3D geometric precondition capturing the volume a frozen skill sweeps during successful executions. Exploiting the insight that the rigidity making a frozen skill fragile at seams also makes its trajectory predictable in advance, we expose each envelope as two classical 3D tools (CHECK to detect blocking residue and FREE to locate safe placement regions), establishing a division of labor where VLAs execute in-distribution manipulation and code repairs out-of-distribution states. On LIBERO-Goal with frozen $π_{0.5}$, our zero-failure envelope predicts collisions at 0.946 AUROC (0.944 from one RGB-D view) and beats frontier VLMs on seam readiness (0.902 vs. 0.793); using it for skill ordering and code repair lifts success from 82.8% to 92.3% on two-step seams and from 29.7% to 93.8% on drawer-bearing 3-4 step chains, matching privileged world reset. Together, these results show that reliable VLA composition hinges on measuring a policy's physical workspace rather than guessing preconditions in language. Ongoing work closes the loop with autonomous LLM orchestration, synthesized free-object recovery, and cross-policy evaluation on cluttered benchmarks and real hardware.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Motion Chain of Thought: Disentangling Motion Semantics from Visual Appearance for Robotic Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World Action Models (WAMs) aim to construct a unified architecture capable of understanding world state evolution and guiding action generation. However, existing visual branches focus on predicting static visual observation, rather than reflecting potential transition information that captures the evolution of world states under motion interactions. This leads to representational entanglement between high-level physical condition evolution and low-level action trajectory generation within the Action Model, creating a structural bottleneck while weakening the predictive capability of world evolution modeling for action generation. We propose PILOT (Physical Inference for Latent Optimized Trajectories), whose core Representational Deduction (RD) bridges this gap by integrating motion chain-of-thought (CoT) guidance as a native model capability. Specifically, RD aims to encourage the action branch to explicitly model potential state transition tokens, which are retained as CoT in the reasoning space to guide fine-grained motion trajectory. Experiments demonstrate that RD not only improves the success rate and generalization ability of WAMs in complex robotic manipulation tasks but also enhances the model's physical interpretability by decoupling high-level motion semantics from low-level trajectory details. Furthermore, the abundant state transition supervision signals introduced by RD effectively alleviate the sparse supervision in action generation, supporting data-efficient real-robot adaptation with reduced demonstration sets.

</details>

![[assets/2611.32238_figure.png|800]]

- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Boundary-Aware Policy Learning for Agent-Orchestrated Robot Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：LingBot-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Agent-orchestrated robot systems combine the general reasoning capability of multimodal agents with the precise physical control of learned visuomotor policies. Yet such systems often fail at the moment of handoff: a policy is reliable only from a limited set of start states, while the agent may invoke it outside this region. We call this region the policy's handoff boundary. Existing whole-task policies typically have handoff boundaries that are narrow and difficult for an agent to characterize, making reliable coordination challenging. We propose Boundary-Aware Policy Learning, which explicitly shapes and then expands the handoff boundary of a visuomotor policy. First, Boundary-Explicit Training fine-tunes the policy on contact segments with diverse start poses, yielding a wide and well-defined boundary that can be communicated to the agent through a simple handoff protocol. Second, Boundary-Expanding Self-Improvement retrains the policy on curated successful system rollouts, extending the boundary toward earlier stages of each task and allowing the agent to hand over sooner with fewer language-model calls. Across RoboTwin 2.0 and RoboDojo, our system outperforms the strongest baseline by over 26 percentage points, reaching 98.4% success in randomized RoboTwin 2.0 scenes, while further reducing its API cost and execution time by 41.3% and 37.5%, respectively. These results suggest that the key to robust agent-policy orchestration lies not only in improving either component individually, but also in explicitly shaping the boundary between them.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Seeing_Is_Not_Reaching_Instruction_Shortcuts_Override_Visual_Grounding_in_Vision-Language-Action_Models|Seeing Is Not Reaching: Instruction Shortcuts Override Visual Grounding in Vision-Language-Action Models]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32206](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32206)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32206

#### 研究背景与动机

《Seeing Is Not Reaching: Instruction Shortcuts Override Visual Grounding in Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA, SmolVLA, TurboVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are expected to inherit visual grounding from vision-language models, yet often reach for an object's training location after it has been moved. Across six VLAs, we identify two shortcuts: a scene shortcut, where the arm follows the scene's original task despite a new instruction, and an instruction shortcut, where the instruction retrieves the requested object's training location. In $π_{0.5}$, the instruction shortcut persists even though object-name attention follows the moved target. We call this Seeing Is Not Reaching. To determine whether this grounding is unused or overridden, we separately mask an image-mediated grounding pathway and a direct instruction-to-action pathway. Masking the grounding pathway eliminates reaches to moved targets, whereas masking the direct pathway increases them. Thus, grounding already guides action but is often overridden by the instruction shortcut, which acts through the direct pathway. We introduce Pathway-Separated Guidance (PSG), which amplifies grounding and suppresses the shortcut without training or external target locations. On LIBERO-PRO Object under Position perturbations, PSG raises the target-reaching rate from 22.0% to 52.6% and task success from 16.6% to 40.8%, while largely preserving performance on the original tasks.

</details>

---

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Reading_Geometric_Anchors_Viewpoint_Generalization_in_Imitation_Learning_for_Robotic_Manipulation_with_RGB-Only_Deployment|Reading Geometric Anchors: Viewpoint Generalization in Imitation Learning for Robotic Manipulation with RGB-Only Deployment]]

![[assets/2611.32162_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32162](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32162)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32162
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Reading_Geometric_Anchors_Viewpoint_Generalization_in_Imitation_Learning_for_Robotic_Manipulation_with_RGB-Only_Deployment|Reading Geometric Anchors: Viewpoint Generalization in Imitation Learning for Robotic Manipulation with RGB-Only Deployment]]
- **作者**: Chengyu Tao, Chunxi Huang
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.3（加权：具身智能 1.2，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL, ComputerVision

#### 研究背景与动机

《Reading Geometric Anchors: Viewpoint Generalization in Imitation Learning for Robotic Manipulation with RGB-Only Deployment》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：FPV-Net, MetaWorld, PointNet, ResNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Moving the camera changes where objects appear in an image, challenging RGB-based imitation learning. A common strategy pairs synthesized views with the same demonstrated action, but ignores supporting physical correspondence across views. By predicting depth with camera calibrations, 3D policies may handle it yet suffer the issue of varied observed point sets. To address these limitations, we propose ReGeoAnch, a geometry-anchored imitation learning framework for viewpoint generalization. Specifically, we build global anchors in the robot base frame and local anchors in the gripper frame, providing shared physical references across viewpoints. Furthermore, Decoupled Appearance-Guided Geometric Readout uses Point-to-Anchor Aggregation (PAA) to obtain comparable and stable geometry around anchors beyond pointwise correspondence. A controllable and decoupled information flow, Anchor-to-Policy Readout (APR), leverages complementary visual and geometric information and supports viewpoint generalization: view-dependent appearances are queried by view-invariant robot state to select task-relevant anchors, with geometric values alone to form the final readout. We further introduce Cross-View Readout Consistency to align the directions of policy readouts from paired source and synthesized target views. Experiments on 12 manipulation tasks across MetaWorld and ManiSkill3 demonstrate improved viewpoint generalization over baseline policies.

</details>
