#### 研究背景与动机

《CoverMem: Coverage-Constrained Visual Memory for Budgeted Embodied Question Answering》归入 具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EM-EQA, EQA, OpenEQA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Episodic-memory embodied question answering enables agents to answer questions based on previously recorded visual observations of the surrounding scenes. However, growing observation histories, varying observation quality across viewpoints, and limited computational resources make it challenging to construct and maintain compact visual memories while retrieving sufficient evidence for question answering. To address these challenges, we introduce CoverMem, which incorporates three key components: (1) a coverage-constrained memory construction method that selects a compact set of informative full frames satisfying object-quality and representative-scene coverage requirements; (2) a coverage-preserving incremental maintenance strategy that locally reselects only the affected frames to satisfy updated coverage requirements as observations arrive; and (3) a budgeted complementary frame selection method that balances question relevance, additional support for question requirements, and redundancy with selected frames within a fixed budget. Experimental results on three benchmarks covering diverse environments and question types demonstrate that CoverMem improves question-answering performance under limited visual budgets.

</details>

![[assets/2611.32101_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Ange Bao, Haishan Zhang, Lingzi Xie, Shuang Li, Pei Zhao, Ling Shi, Bidan Huang
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 强化学习

#### 研究背景与动机

《Ego2Real: Learning Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation from Egocentric Visuo-Tactile Demonstrations》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Learning dexterous manipulation from egocentric human demonstrations offers a scalable path toward robot skill acquisition. Vision alone, however, provides limited information about physical contacts and the demonstrator's force intent, leading to ambiguities in interpreting hand-object interactions. Capturing human touch via tactile sensing is therefore crucial for reliable human-to-robot dexterous skill transfer. We present Ego2Real, a framework that leverages egocentric demonstrations and tactile gloves to transfer bimanual dexterous manipulation skills to robotic hands. Ego2Real incorporates tactile supervision at two complementary stages: (1) tactile-guided hand-object interaction reconstruction, which uses measured tactile signals to refine hand poses and contact geometry; and (2) contact-informed reinforcement learning, which translates human contact patterns into robotic contact rewards. By combining refined motion references with physical contact rewards during training, Ego2Real guides anthropomorphic robotic hands to emulate demonstrated contact behaviors while adapting to their own morphology and actuation. Experiments show that incorporating tactile supervision into both reconstruction and policy learning yields higher manipulation success rates, underscoring the importance of enriching egocentric demonstrations with tactile observations to improve their quality and transferability.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《KeyMemVLA: Subtask-Guided Key Memory for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ChainVLA, EventVLA, KMBench, KeyMemVLA, MIKASA-Robo-VLA, MemoryBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Memory-dependent long-horizon manipulation requires vision-language-action (VLA) policies to infer task progress and recall evidence absent from current observations. Under a limited memory budget, the challenge is to retain history that resolves stage ambiguity and supports subsequent decisions. We present KeyMemVLA, a subtask-guided key memory framework based on progress indication and decision-evidence retention. A two-level annotation process selects subtasks satisfying either criterion and labels their final frames as keyframes to supervise online memory writes. A shared vision-language backbone jointly learns subtask, action, and keyframe prediction. At inference, the policy first predicts the current subtask from the instruction, visual context, and memory, then uses this semantic condition to predict actions and future memory-write positions. Actual observations are retained at the selected times during execution. We introduce KMBench to evaluate active memory retention for decisions across subtasks. Evaluations on RMBench-$M(n)$, RoboTwin-MeM, KMBench, and real-world manipulation tasks show higher average success rates than the compared baselines. Ablation studies and representation analysis support the roles of key memory and explicit task-progress semantics. These results highlight the value of coupling sparse key memory with online progress inference for history-dependent control.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/EffectWAM_Learning_Action-Grounded_Effects_for_Robot_Manipulation|EffectWAM: Learning Action-Grounded Effects for Robot Manipulation]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《EffectWAM: Learning Action-Grounded Effects for Robot Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World-action models connect visual prediction with executable robot control. We present EffectWAM, which develops this connection through three complementary components: Effect, camera positional encoding (CaPE), and Action Grounding. Effect represents visual change as the difference between independently encoded future observations and a shared current-observation anchor. CaPE conditions endpoint pooling on relative camera pose when reliable extrinsics are available, while sharing the same learned parameters with pose-free pooling for uncalibrated trajectories. Action Grounding trains the visual representation to retain measured changes in end-effector pose and gripper state. An exponential moving average of the observation encoder supplies Effect targets. A shared generation expert learns to jointly predict Effect and an executable action chunk through flow matching, and reuses the sampled pair to generate future video on demand. This allows the controller to execute actions without waiting for video synthesis. We train on more than 20,000 hours of heterogeneous embodied data, excluding unused chain-of-thought data, and accommodate dataset-specific joint, end-effector, and hand-pose representations. Our evaluation uses the Small (8B) configuration on all five benchmarks and additionally evaluates the Large (26B) configuration on RoboDojo. Experiments examine closed-loop manipulation across complementary benchmarks, and comparisons across multiple baseline policies show consistent improvements from Action Grounding.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《RoboHarness-RL: Agentic Reinforcement Learning for VLM-Based Robot Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AgenticRoboRL, CaP-RL, Contact-GraspNet, RoboHarness-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Agentic harnesses enable frontier vision-language models (VLMs) to manipulate robots through perception, planning, and control tools, yet achieving comparable performance with compact open-weight VLMs remains challenging. We present RoboHarness-RL, an agentic reinforcement learning framework for improving VLM-based robot manipulation through tool calls. Starting from supervised fine-tuning on successful teacher demonstrations, the VLM further improves through multi-turn reinforcement learning with task-outcome rewards, without relying on vision-language-action models or world action models. To support efficient multi-turn training, we develop LiteHarness, which uses hierarchical memory to keep interaction contexts compact while preserving task performance. With Qwen3.5-9B, RL raises success from 31.2% to 44.0% on RoboTwin 2.0 and from 57.8% to 73.3% on LIBERO-Pro; these gains extend to randomized RoboTwin scenes unseen during training (26.4% to 38.0%). Behavioral analysis further shows that RL reduces repetitive-call rates by up to $4\times$, shortens successful executions, and improves recovery from negative feedback. Project page: https://aim-uofa.github.io/RoboHarness-RL/

</details>

- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.5（加权：具身智能 1.8，机器人 0.7）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Perception Is Enough, at the Right Scale: Scale-Adaptive Token Pruning for Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EcoVLA, EffVLA, EfficientVLA, OpenVLA, SP-VLA, SmolVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) models must process continuous streams of visual observations, and the resulting computational cost limits real-time robotic control. Token pruning reduces this cost, but a fixed semantic scale of perceptual representation can overlook information needed at different stages of manipulation. We show that perceptual semantics can guide effective token selection without relying on internal attention when the selection scale adapts to the scene's evolving semantic structure. Based on this observation, we introduce TauPrune-VLA, a training-free method that controls the scale of the retained perceptual representation through a single perception temperature $τ$. To adapt this scale as manipulation proceeds, TauPrune-VLA updates $τ$ online using trends in perceptual semantics, with robot-state changes and action magnitude regulating the adjustment strength. While these semantics evolve over the course of manipulation, they remain relatively stable between consecutive frames. This continuity allows retention indices computed from the current observation to be applied at the next policy invocation, enabling token selection to run asynchronously with inference and reducing pruning overhead. Evaluated across diverse VLA models and simulation benchmarks, TauPrune-VLA achieves up to a $1.86\times$ inference speedup while maintaining near-baseline task success rates. We further validate its effectiveness on two real-world robotic platforms.

</details>

- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《DUALingo: A Dual-Hand Instruction-Guided Dataset for Affordance Grounding on 3D Objects》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AffordanceNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Affordance grounding localizes where an agent should interact with an object to perform a task. Many everyday manipulation tasks, however, require bimanual affordance, in which two hands assume complementary and cooperative roles to accomplish a shared goal. Existing 3D affordance datasets primarily ground single interaction regions, and manually annotating dual-hand, cooperative contacts does not scale. To address this gap, we introduce DUALingo, a large-scale dataset for task-conditioned, role-aware bimanual 3D affordance grounding on diverse everyday objects. DUALingo represents each task as two cooperative, hand-agnostic roles, each grounded as a heatmap on the object surface. It contains 271K free-form tasks on 71K objects, generated by a fully automated pipeline of open-weight foundation models without human labeling. Expert ratings and a validated LLM judge confirm the quality of the resulting annotations. Trained on DUALingo, existing 3D affordance models learn to ground both cooperative roles from natural-language instructions, with the best-performing model achieving a more than threefold improvement in aIoU over the zero-shot baseline, from 10.3 to 37.8. Robotic manipulation demonstrations illustrate the actionability of the predicted bimanual affordances in performing cooperative tasks. These results position DUALingo as a scalable foundation for learning everyday bimanual affordances. Project page: https://nycu-pair-labs.github.io/DUALingo/

</details>

- **作者**: Ziyang Chen, Dehua Chen, Shaoguang Wang, Xu Yan, Hao Huang, Tao Chen
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Beyond Expert Replay: Cross-Instruction Anchoring for Continual Vision-Language-Action Learning》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CoT-VLA, Info-VLA, OpenVLA, QwenVLA, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) models need to continually acquire new manipulation skills while retaining the ability to follow previously learned instructions. Experience replay (ER) can mitigate catastrophic forgetting, but retaining only a small number of historical expert frames per task leaves many old-instruction responses without direct supervision. Under limited replay, we observe a structured failure mode in which an earlier instruction can trigger a subsequently learned, competing goal. To address this gap, we propose Cross-Instruction Anchoring (XIA), which supplements expert replay with instruction-conditioned response preservation. At each task boundary, XIA freezes the previous policy as a teacher and matches its responses to old instructions paired with current observations. This extends supervision beyond stored historical examples without requiring additional expert replay data. Experiments across multiple VLA architectures and three LIBERO suites demonstrate improved success rates and reduced forgetting compared with ER under identical historical expert-data budgets. Additional RoboCasa and real-world experiments further support the effectiveness of XIA.

</details>

![[assets/2611.31894_figure.png|800]]

- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《RESET: One Demonstration, One Verified World, Self-Improved Skills Across Embodiments》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：D-World, Re3Sim, Real-World, Real-to-Sim, X-Sim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large language model (LLM) coding agents can iteratively self-improve robot manipulation skills in simulation benchmarks, yet real-world tasks provide neither ground-truth success scorers nor resettable practice environments. Our key observation is that a single real demonstration simultaneously specifies scene geometry, defines the terminal success criterion, and serves as a physical reference trajectory for verifying world fidelity---all decoupled from the source robot's kinematics. We present {RESET}: we reconstruct a contact-rich world and derive a frozen read-only criterion from one demonstration ({R}ebuild); check that world against the demonstration by open-loop replay and silhouette consistency ({E}xamine); evolve modular white-box task graphs and skill code from per-node failure evidence ({S}elf-improve); deploy frozen code on real robots with zero runtime LLM calls ({E}xecute); and share the verified world and criterion with unseen robots without new demonstrations ({T}ransfer). On 8 manipulation tasks, each rebuilt from one real demonstration, RESET achieves 97% average success on unseen layouts in simulation, whereas zero-shot code reaches 27%, per-task fine-tuned pi0.5 policies 31%, and Diffusion Policy 50%; injecting reconstruction defects shows that practice tolerates many reconstruction errors, that geometric errors can still stall or mislead it, and that the check flags most of them while physical randomization covers the rest. Without any target demonstrations, 3 heterogeneous robots acquire the same task skills in the shared world: a robot with similar arms copies 95% of its skill functions unchanged from the source robot, while robots with different arms and hands write their own. In real-robot experiments, the resulting programs run on 4 robots and succeed in 80% of 280 trials.

</details>

- **作者**: Furui Xu, Weishuai Zeng, Zirui Wang, Kangning Yin, Jiacheng Bao, Huayi Wang, Yingying Qin, Yufei Xue, Yang Tang, Zenghao Tang, Kailin Li, Jingbo Wang...

#### 研究背景与动机

《Ada-BFM: Adapting Behavior Foundation Models for Humanoid Scene Interaction》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose humanoid control through pretraining on large-scale behavioral data. However, their proficiency in whole-body coordination does not readily extend to scene interaction, which requires adapting motion to scene-dependent geometric constraints and contact dynamics. To bridge this gap, we introduce Ada-BFM, a post-training framework that adapts pretrained BFMs for humanoid scene interaction. Ada-BFM augments the original architecture with a lightweight adapter, enabling interaction-specific adjustments while leveraging pretrained control capabilities. Rather than producing a separate controller for each task, the framework yields a shared model for each interaction domain, supporting diverse tasks involving terrain or object interaction. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate that Ada-BFM enables diverse scene interaction behaviors, accelerates learning compared with training from scratch, and largely preserves pretrained behavioral competence. These findings establish Ada-BFM as an effective and efficient approach to extending the capabilities of BFMs beyond their pretraining settings while retaining their strengths in general whole-body control.

</details>

![[assets/2611.31850_first_page.png|800]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《Two-Stage Post-Training for Flow-Based Embodied Foundation Models》归入 具身智能、强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DSRL, Meta-World, SimpleVLA-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large-scale pretraining provides embodied models with general capabilities, but reliable task execution still requires post training. In multitask settings, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) faces distribution mismatch between training and deployment, whereas reinforcement learning (RL) is limited by sparse rewards and uneven learning progress across tasks. Multi-teacher on policy distillation (MOPD) consolidates expert capabilities through dense supervision on the model's own trajectories, but achieving performance beyond the teachers remains challenging. We propose a two-stage post training framework for flow-based embodied models, constructing distillation objectives from the velocity regression formulation of conditional flow matching. Stage 1 efficiently consolidates expert capabilities through MOPD. Stage 2 combines RL with teacher distillation that weights velocity errors according to the sampling noise, where distillation is restricted to failed trajectories to supplement sparse rewards with dense guidance without directly constraining successful behaviors. Experiments across multiple models and benchmarks show that the framework outperforms joint SFT and exceeds the corresponding teachers' aggregate success rates. Sample efficiency comparisons show higher success rates than direct RL with fewer environment interactions.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reactive robotic manipulation is acutely sensitive to simultaneous sensing noise, measurement dropouts, joint execution errors, and contact parameter variations. While single-frame frameworks like Domain-Randomized Instance Set (DRIS) achieve strong robustness under isolated uncertainties (86.4%), they degrade severely when perception and actuation uncertainties co-occur (27.6% across 10 coupled stress scenarios) due to memoryless encoding and fixed high-stiffness PD control. We propose Co-DRIS + SuperMimic, a perception-action co-design framework coupling: (i) an inertial world-frame physics-informed world model combining exact ballistic kinematics with a zero-initialized contact residual to roll forward missing observations and accelerate training via zero-cost counterfactual dropout consistency; (ii) an action-conditioned dual-stream causal GRU belief encoder resolving the denoising-vs-lag deadlock; and (iii) phase-aware variable-impedance soft-catching that transitions the 7-DoF Franka Research 3 arm into an overdamped regime (zeta/zeta0 ~ 2.26x) at the torque layer. Under unbiased 3-seed tail evaluation on unseen throws (15,360 throws/scenario), our method achieves 89.6 +/- 0.4% on standard benchmarks (+10.5% on 4-sigma noise to 70.5%) and 66.6 +/- 0.5% across 10 coupled/dropout stress scenarios (+39.0% over DRIS; +30.4% on 50% dropout). We further validate impedance decoupling, causal contact triggering under +0.65 s flight-time shifts, and cross-task transfer to steel-plate ping-pong bouncing (87.5% to 100.0%) and FR3+Hand catching (+8.4% under 4x noise with 6-13% lower peak impact force).

</details>

![[assets/2611.31782_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Jin Yang, Youlin Wang, Ping Wei
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 大模型, 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《Many Senses, One World: Learning Hierarchical Multimodal World Representations for Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation》归入 具身智能、大模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、大模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MoDE-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Bimanual dexterous manipulation relies on multimodal inputs that capture visual context and non-visual physical interaction cues for precise and coordinated control. To address the challenge of effectively integrating these modalities for policy learning, we propose HiMoDex, a two-stage framework centered on hierarchical multimodal world-state representations. In Stage 1, we pretrain a hierarchical representation network on the collected dataset of 1.19 million synchronized multimodal time steps. The network integrates visual context with tactile, force-torque, and audio cues into unified world-state embeddings. In Stage 2, we freeze the pretrained network and use its embeddings to condition a shared world-action transformer. The transformer jointly learns action generation and action-conditioned future-state prediction. Experiments across five real-world tasks show that HiMoDex achieves an average success rate of 78.7\%, representing an absolute improvement of 32.0\% over the strongest evaluated baseline. Extensive ablations validate the benefits of hierarchical representation learning and two-stage training. Failure analysis helps explain performance differences across variants. Code, data, and demos will be released at https://anonymous.4open.science/w/research-website-BAB4/.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/LeanWAM_Learning_Latent_World_Action_Models_from_Frozen_Video_Models|LeanWAM: Learning Latent World Action Models from Frozen Video Models]]

![[assets/2611.31740_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.31740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31740)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.31740
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/LeanWAM_Learning_Latent_World_Action_Models_from_Frozen_Video_Models|LeanWAM: Learning Latent World Action Models from Frozen Video Models]]
- **作者**: Shaolong Li, Hongze Fu, Joyce Chai, Yinpei Dai
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 1.7（加权：具身智能 1.2，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《LeanWAM: Learning Latent World Action Models from Frozen Video Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：InternVLA, OpenVLA, PDRVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World action models (WAMs) typically adapt large pretrained video generators to jointly predict future observations and robot actions, incurring substantial training and deployment costs. However, it remains unclear which future representations a policy actually needs and how compactly they can be represented. Our probing study shows that complementary future representations can be compressed into a few tokens while retaining most of their action-decoding performance. Motivated by this finding, we introduce LeanWAM, a latent WAM that transfers video priors from a frozen video generator to a lightweight policy via two-stage training. In the first stage, a Future Extractor compresses observed transitions into 32 predictive tokens. These tokens condition the frozen generator and are jointly shaped by action, RGB, semantic, and geometric supervision, while a compact readout of the generator's hidden states supplies complementary world tokens. The second stage distills both representations into the policy, which learns to predict them from current observations while generating actions. With 1.4B parameters and no large-scale embodied pretraining, LeanWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin~2.0. It predicts an action chunk in 25.1\,ms on an A100 GPU, 12.3--41.5$\times$ faster than representative WAMs. These results demonstrate that compact predictive supervision can transfer video priors to robust and efficient control.

</details>

![[assets/2611.31689_figure.png|800]]

- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.31689
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/GeoMVGen_Joint_Appearance-Geometry_Modeling_for_Multi-View_Video_Generation_in_Robotic_Manipulation|GeoMVGen: Joint Appearance-Geometry Modeling for Multi-View Video Generation in Robotic Manipulation]]
- **作者**: Qiaoqiao Jia, Guile Wu, Dongfeng Bai
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《GeoMVGen: Joint Appearance-Geometry Modeling for Multi-View Video Generation in Robotic Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robotic manipulation requires predicting how robot-object interactions evolve over time, encompassing both visual appearance and scene geometry. In multi-camera settings, such predictions need to capture the evolving 3D scene consistently across viewpoints, while supporting controllable generation under different robot actions and camera configurations. Existing video generation models provide strong priors for visual appearance and motion but typically lack explicit geometric modeling. Meanwhile, recent advances in geometry-aware and multi-view generation have explored joint appearance-geometry modeling and cross-view consistency, yet generating temporally coherent appearance and geometry across multiple viewpoints under robotic manipulation conditions remains challenging. In this work, we propose GeoMVGen, a framework for joint multi-view RGB and pointmap video generation in robotic manipulation. Built on a video diffusion transformer, our framework employs modality-specific adaptation modules to capture the distinct characteristics of appearance and geometry, while using cross-modal residual connections to facilitate information exchange between the two modalities. To establish geometric correspondences across viewpoints, we further introduce a cross-view projection mechanism with complementary geometric supervisions, combining pointmap consistency with geometry foundation model-guided feature alignment. These designs enable controllable generation of temporally evolving multi-view observations with coherent appearance and geometrically consistent scene structures. Extensive experiments on 4D video generation dataset comprising simulated and real-world robotic manipulation scenarios demonstrate that GeoMVGen outperforms state-of-the-art methods in video quality, geometric accuracy, and cross-view consistency.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/One_Phase_Is_Enough_Sim-to-Real_Transfer_with_Direct_Visual_Reinforcement_Learning|One Phase Is Enough: Sim-to-Real Transfer with Direct Visual Reinforcement Learning]]

![[assets/2611.31588_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.31588](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31588)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.31588

- **作者**: John Marangola, Antonia Bronars, Younghyo Park, Stephen Andrews, Kushal Kedia, Pulkit Agrawal
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《One Phase Is Enough: Sim-to-Real Transfer with Direct Visual Reinforcement Learning》归入 具身智能、强化学习、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、强化学习、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) has enabled impressive robotic behaviors, yet deploying policies directly from vision remains challenging. A common approach first trains a teacher policy with RL using privileged state observations, then distills its behavior into a vision-based student via imitation. However, the resulting student can substantially underperform its teacher. Directly training visual policies with RL offers a promising alternative, but learning task-relevant visual representations from scratch can be sample-inefficient, making one-phase visual RL intractable. We propose DRIFT, a simple recipe for efficient one-phase visual RL using image observations available at test time. Our key insight is to use frozen, pretrained foundation vision encoders, allowing RL to focus on learning control from existing visual representations. Freezing the encoder avoids costly backpropagation through the vision backbone, while its pretrained representations enable real-world transfer from fast, rasterized simulation rendering. Under matched architectures, observations, and compute budgets, DRIFT outperforms two-phase distillation and achieves a 58.8% relative improvement in average real-world success rate across three manipulation tasks. Controlled simulation evaluations demonstrate that DRIFT's relative advantage grows from roughly 9% in-distribution to over 61% under out-of-distribution shifts, suggesting that stronger generalization contributes to superior real-world transfer. Project website: https://jmarangola.github.io/one-phase-visual-rl/

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《FabricBench: A Standardized Policy Benchmark for Bimanual Fabric Manipulation with a Contact-Driven Deformable Simulator》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Fabric manipulation challenges robot policies with changing geometry, self-occlusion, and contact, yet standardized evaluation remains focused on rigid objects. We introduce FabricBench, a benchmark for bimanual fabric manipulation with six tasks spanning garments, silk, and bags, 10,000 simulation-ready assets, and controlled tests of configuration and appearance generalization. Built on an open-source solver in Newton, our simulator enables stable bimanual grasping, holding, and release through contact forces, without artificial attachment. Redesigned cloth-rigid contact and bidirectional force coupling support interactions among robot arms, fabric, and free rigid objects, allowing a bag to support its contents and transmit their weight to the grippers. In a controlled grasp-hold-release evaluation, our solver succeeds in all 45 trials, compared with none for the tested baseline configurations. Evaluating six representative policies, including vision-language-action models and world action models, reveals substantial remaining challenges: the best policy achieves 77% average success, while no policy exceeds 36% on long-horizon T-shirt folding. Under the most deformed initial T-shirt configurations, success falls to 6-14% across policies, with larger drops than under the evaluated appearance shifts. Zero-shot deployment of $π_{0.5}$ on a real robot largely preserves task difficulty ordering across six tasks (Spearman $ρ=0.94$). These results establish FabricBench as a reproducible testbed for measuring fabric-manipulation capabilities and exposing limitations in configuration generalization. The project website is available at https://fabricbench.github.io/.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Hi3R-VLA, OpenVLA, PointWorld, RLBench, Real-World, SpatialVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

While recent vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly incorporate 3D geometry, how to effectively exploit it for precise and generalizable action prediction remains a central challenge. We introduce Hi3R-VLA, a 3D policy that grounds heterogeneous modalities - vision, language, and action - in explicit 3D geometry for robotic manipulation. Our model integrates spatially-aligned visual features and language conditioning into point cloud representations, enriching scene geometry with fine-grained, task-relevant semantics. It then anchors low-level control commands in the same 3D coordinate frame and uses 3D-RoPE enhanced action-to-point attention over coarse-to-fine point features to model geometric relationships between action targets and observed points. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of semantic fusion within 3D and spatial alignment between observations and actions. With fewer than 250M trainable parameters, Hi3R-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance on RoboCasa, RLBench, RoboTwin2, DexJoCo and DexSuite across diverse manipulation tasks and embodiments, from single-arm and dual-arm grippers to dexterous hands. Real-robot evaluations further demonstrate its effectiveness in the physical world. The model, code and dataset are publicly released at https://cshizhe.github.io/projects/hi3r_vla.html.

</details>

![[assets/2611.31511_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.31511](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31511)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.31511
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Near-Zero_Forgetting_Is_Not_Enough_Out-of-Distribution_Robustness_in_Continual_Learning_of_Pretrained_VLAs|Near-Zero Forgetting Is Not Enough: Out-of-Distribution Robustness in Continual Learning of Pretrained VLAs]]
- **作者**: Adrià Ruiz, Bartłomiej Twardowski, Tinne Tuytelaars, Sergi Masip
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

- **关联关键词**: Multimodal

#### 研究背景与动机

《Near-Zero Forgetting Is Not Enough: Out-of-Distribution Robustness in Continual Learning of Pretrained VLAs》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ExpReS-VLA, ImageNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recent work shows that pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit near-zero forgetting in specific continual learning settings with experience replay. However, robustness to domain shift of learned tasks remains unexplored. In this work, we study the robustness of Pi0 fine-tuned with experience replay under out-of-distribution (OOD) data using the LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks. While the in-domain (ID) setup achieves near-zero catastrophic forgetting, OOD performance on previous tasks quickly collapses. Specifically, we show that during the continual learning process, the OOD capabilities of the model degrade, following a shared representational pattern across domain shifts, which is also visible behaviorally in the two simpler suites. Representational analysis shows that drift concentrates in the multimodal and action features. Linear probes indicate that OOD features still retain task-relevant information, but appear unreadable, consistent with representation misalignment. These findings suggest that even when ID stability remains intact, severe OOD forgetting can still occur, accompanied by representational drift during continual learning.

</details>

![[assets/2611.31488_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Ruwan Wickramarachchi, Jonathan Francis, Alessandro Oltramari
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Embodied Neuro-symbolic AI: Making Robot Behavior Auditable, Safe, and Scalable》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) end-to-end systems have driven major progress in robot learning, yet they still face persistent barriers to dependable real-world deployment. In practice, these models often exhibit brittle generalization under distribution shift (i.e., novel objects, layouts, lighting, or contact conditions), limited physical and causal reasoning, and inconsistent fine-grained motor control. Critically, many VLA pipelines offer limited ways to express and audit what a robot must not do, making it difficult to provide assurance in safety-critical settings. These limitations constrain scalability, maintainability, and trust when robots move beyond controlled demonstrations. We propose Embodied Neuro-Symbolic (NeSy) AI: a deployment-oriented paradigm that integrates learned perception, planning and control with explicit, machine-readable background knowledge and formal constraints. By combining knowledge-guided perception, constraint-aware symbolic planning, and hybrid learning, Embodied NeSy AI enables robots to reason about their environment, adapt to new tasks, and provide auditable, machine-checkable compliance with safety and operational requirements, thus supporting scalable, trustworthy robot deployment.

</details>

![[assets/2611.31452_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Guangting Zheng, Shaoxuan Wang, Yu Yuan, Zhipeng Tang, Jiajun Deng, Yanyong Zhang
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Ground, Guide, Act: Improving VLA Spatial Generalization with Multimodal Foundation Models》归入 具身智能、大模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、大模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ALOHA-Sim, MFM-VLA, OpenVLA, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models effectively learn action policies from robot demonstrations, but struggle to generalize to changes in object positions and task goals. General multimodal foundation models (MFMs) offer complementary scene understanding and spatial reasoning, but remain slow and unreliable for direct fine-grained control. To understand how MFMs can improve VLA spatial generalization, we conduct a systematic capability analysis across multiple VLA architectures using atomic-stage interventions, revealing that many failures stem from poor target grounding and global spatial routing, while local manipulation skills remain largely usable. Based on these findings, we propose MFM-VLA, a selective collaboration framework in which an MFM provides target grounding and spatial guidance, controllers execute free-space motion, and the VLA retains control over fine-grained manipulation. We fine-tune the VLA with action-chunk-level dense geometric captions and perturbed-state recovery data to improve local guidance following and recovery from unfamiliar handoff states. A development-calibrated capability router selects between direct VLA execution and MFM-assisted execution to avoid unnecessary intervention. Across LIBERO-PRO, ALOHA-Sim-PRO, RoboCasa365, and real-world chemistry tasks, MFM-VLA improves success rates by 14.9, 74.0, 3.6, and 30.9 percentage points, respectively, over the corresponding base VLA. The project page is available at https://tom-zgt.github.io/MFM-VLA

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/ASTERISM_Do_Multimodal_LLMs_Actually_Look_at_All_the_Images|ASTERISM: Do Multimodal LLMs Actually Look at All the Images?]]

![[assets/2611.32316_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32316](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32316)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32316
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/ASTERISM_Do_Multimodal_LLMs_Actually_Look_at_All_the_Images|ASTERISM: Do Multimodal LLMs Actually Look at All the Images?]]
- **作者**: Chanseul Cho, Dongwoo Kim, Noori Bae, Seungjae Moon, Seongsu Lee, Youngmin Ro
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型, 强化学习, 世界模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.37（加权：大模型 0.85，强化学习 0.36，世界模型 0.16）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Multimodal, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《ASTERISM: Do Multimodal LLMs Actually Look at All the Images?》归入 大模型、强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MuirBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Multi-image benchmarks aim to test whether a multimodal large language model (MLLM) combines evidence across images, but many of their items can be answered from a single image. Across about 33k items from 19 public benchmarks, our audit and a two-stage single-image filter find that one image or the question alone settles 24.2% of them. On these items, MLLMs recover from one image a median 78% of the accuracy that all images add over the text alone, so these items reward answering from one image, which we call the single-image shortcut. MLLMs take this shortcut even on items where one image is not enough. On such items, most of their wrong answers repeat an answer they give from one image, at a rate well above chance. We build ASTERISM, to our knowledge the largest audited multi-image benchmark, from these 19 benchmarks by keeping their items that need more than one image. Its 18.7k items, 2.0k of them corrected or rewritten, share one format, one scorer and seven task categories. We also release ASTERISM-Train with 49k training items and ASTERISM-Lite, a hard subset whose items we manually verify one by one. On ASTERISM-Lite, the strongest proprietary MLLM scores 68%. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), now common in MLLM post-training, rewards the shortcut as well, because a correct answer read off one image earns as much reward as one that combines images. In line with this, existing RLVR objectives add little to the ASTERISM score. To stop rewarding the shortcut in training as ASTERISM does in evaluation, we propose cross-image credit assignment (CICA), an extension of RLVR that moves the credit toward the tokens that need more than one image. On three open-source MLLMs, CICA raises the ASTERISM score by 2.6 to 6.0 points, more than twice the gain of each general and vision-specific RL objective trained on the same data.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/ARS-VLM_Visually_Aligned_Augmented_Random_Search_for_Vision-Language_Models|ARS-VLM: Visually Aligned Augmented Random Search for Vision-Language Models]]

![[assets/2611.32147_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Xiangwei Wang, Jianyuan Lin, Peng Wang, Saman Halgamuge
- **cs 子类**: cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型, 强化学习, 世界模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.37（加权：大模型 0.65，强化学习 0.56，世界模型 0.16）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, RL, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《ARS-VLM: Visually Aligned Augmented Random Search for Vision-Language Models》归入 大模型、强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computation and Language 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

First-order reinforcement learning, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), is widely used to post-train Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for multimodal reasoning. However, it incurs heavy memory and system overhead from backpropagation, optimizer states, and a reference model. Forward-only zeroth-order (ZO) methods, such as Evolution Strategies (ES) and its top-k variant Augmented Random Search (ARS), avoid these overheads via reward-guided weight perturbations, yet remain unexplored for VLM reasoning. We find that directly applying standard ES or vanilla ARS to VLMs suffers from two fundamental failure modes: (i) patch-grid overfitting, fitting fragile grid-specific visual token artifacts while ignoring spatial invariants, and (ii) catastrophic forgetting, degrading general perception and out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks due to unbounded high-rank parameter and policy drift. We propose ARS-VLM, a visually aligned Augmented Random Search framework for forward-only VLM post-training. First, ARS-VLM extracts a contrastive visual basis from bilinearly paired cross-grid views, concentrating perturbations on shared visual signals while suppressing patch-shift artifacts. Second, ARS-VLM anchors shallow visual-fusion layers with a soft subspace prior while freeing deep reasoning layers, restraining policy drift without a reference model. Third, ARS-VLM scores perturbations on paired views to discard view-fragile directions during top-k selection. We evaluate ARS-VLM on Qwen3-VL-4B and Qwen3-VL-8B on multimodal reasoning tasks. On 8B, ARS-VLM reaches 61.0% validation accuracy in 20 steps versus 150 for GRPO (7.5x fewer optimization steps) and avoids the late OOD collapse of vanilla ARS and ES. Moreover, ARS-VLM achieves 75.41% single-view micro accuracy across seven OOD vision reasoning benchmarks, compared with 75.31% for full-parameter GRPO, using forward-only post-training.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

The training of socially intelligent language models often relies on large language model (LLM) judges, which are expensive, noisy, and vulnerable to reward hacking. Multi-player social games offer a natural alternative: they require open-ended communication to coordinate with teammates and infer hidden roles, while their rules provide an objective outcome which can serve as a verifiable reward for training. Using three social games, we explore how much social intelligence Qwen3 models can acquire through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Results show that against a much stronger Gemma-3-27B model, SFT and RLVR raise the win rate of Qwen3-4B from 33% to 81% on the three trained games, and from 39% to 54% on three additional held-out games. In contrast, in-context strategies such as prompting improve the win rate of Qwen3-4B by only about 6 pp across the same games we train and evaluate on. Furthermore, we show that the gains from SFT and RLVR hold across the model sizes 1.7B, 4B, and 32B. Qualitative analyses of agent behavior show signs of stronger social intelligence; for example, trained agents tend to share information more openly, check each other's claims against public evidence, and coordinate their votes. More broadly, our results demonstrate that training LLMs on verifiable outcomes in social games can cultivate generalizable social behaviors, which offers a scalable path for building socially intelligent agents.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/fork()_for_Agents_Agent_Kernel_for_Non-blocking_LLM_Orchestration|fork() for Agents: Agent Kernel for Non-blocking LLM Orchestration]]

![[assets/2611.31727_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.31727](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31727)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.31727
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/fork()_for_Agents_Agent_Kernel_for_Non-blocking_LLM_Orchestration|fork() for Agents: Agent Kernel for Non-blocking LLM Orchestration]]
- **作者**: Ankit Chadha, Pratyay Banerjee
- **cs 子类**: cs.CL, cs.DC
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.35（加权：大模型 1.35）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《fork() for Agents: Agent Kernel for Non-blocking LLM Orchestration》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computation and Language 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AutomationBench, InterruptBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

LLM-based agents typically execute tool calls synchronously and discard completed work upon failure. Subgraph-selective recovery, re-executing only a failed node's transitive descendants, is a well-established pattern in distributed systems (Spark, Erlang/OTP, Dryad), and recent agent runtimes (Sherlock, AgentGit, Atomix) preserve completed work through verifiers, version control, or transactional tool boundaries. We present \textbf{Agent-Kernel}, a non-blocking runtime that ports this pattern to the LLM \emph{content plane}: its primitive, \textbf{Sub-Graph Recovery (SGR)}, fires on a raw exception with no trained verifier or planner, resets a failed node's descendants while preserving completed siblings, and composes each parent's text into its children's prompts (context inheritance), all on a durable substrate with a formal state-preservation guarantee. Across 14{,}434 runs on synthetic and standard benchmarks (tau-bench, Gaia2, AutomationBench, InterruptBench), SGR reaches full recovery within budget=3 where whole-trajectory replay exhausts (100\% vs.\ 55\%), and is roughly 10$\times$ faster than an adapted Sherlock rollback that also recovers. It matches ReAct-serial accuracy within noise on linear workloads while adding recovery and parallelism (up to 12.7$\times$ speedup) where structure permits. Ablation isolates SGR: removing it collapses success to 39.4\%.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《RECAP: Training Long-horizon LLM Agents with Context Compaction》归入 大模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Context compaction enables large language model agents to tackle long-horizon tasks within limited context budgets by replacing interaction histories with compressed summaries. However, learning effective compaction jointly with the agent policy remains challenging, as summaries affect task success over many subsequent interactions. We introduce RECAP, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework based on the principle that a summary should preserve the behavior induced by the original interaction history. We formalize this principle with a behavior distortion objective that measures the divergence between the agent's subsequent behavior under compressed and original contexts. We show that the gradient of the distortion objective decomposes into two complementary learning signals: distortion-based reward for summary generation and self-distillation for subsequent actions. Experiments on Qwen3.5-4B show that RECAP consistently outperforms the task-reward-only RL baselines on both environments, leading to an absolute improvement of 4.6% and 4.3% on BrowseComp-plus and SWE-bench Verified, respectively. Ablations further highlight the value of jointly supervising summary generation and post-compaction behavior for RECAP.

</details>

- **作者**: Richmond Sin Jing Xuan, Peilin Feng, Wenbin Wang, Soujanya Poria
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Lazy Agents Answer Against Their Own Beliefs: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Beliefs Teaches Them to Act on What They Believe》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Artificial Intelligence 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

AI agents working on long tasks often commit to an answer before they have enough evidence, a behavior called laziness. In our evaluations, they frequently commit even while their stated belief still names several possible answers. An outcome reward at the end of the episode scores only the final answer, so it cannot teach the agent when to commit, because a lucky guess receives the same reward as an answer backed by evidence. During training, however, the simulator can compute the exact posterior over the answer at every step and tell whether the evidence is already enough. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Beliefs (RLVB), which rewards the agent for taking further steps while this posterior still allows several answers and for committing once the evidence is sufficient. The reward needs no judge, critic or reward model, and it never reads the belief line the agent writes before each action. We also introduce Pro Worlds, a benchmark of visual puzzles where the agent views a large world through a small window. We evaluate three vision-language models on three benchmarks, two of which are never seen in training. RLVB improves the base model in all nine settings, by 17.1% on average. Under the outcome reward, GRPO and PPO fall below the base model on average. RLVB also outperforms PPO with a process reward from an LLM judge by 9.3% on average while using 1.6 times fewer GPU-hours. The trained agent answers with sufficient evidence in 51%, 12% and 74% of the episodes on the three benchmarks, against 8%, 0.4% and 36% for the base model. Its accuracy gain therefore comes from answering more often at high levels of evidence. The belief line shows how the agent reaches this evidence: it acts on its own belief. It commits at only 0.1 to 2.2% of the turns at which the evidence is insufficient and its line still names several answers, against 3 to 68% for the base model.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Breaking the Joint Action Space Dependence in Distributionally Robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Q-FTRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We study the sample complexity of learning robust coarse correlated equilibria (CCE) in finite-horizon, general-sum, distributionally robust Markov games with known rewards and a generative model of the nominal transitions. Each player evaluates a policy by its worst-case return over total-variation (TV) balls centered at the nominal transition distribution of every stage, state, and joint action; this is the standard $(s,a)$-rectangular uncertainty set. Existing generative-model guarantees for this model scale with the number $\prod_iA_i$ of joint actions, where $A_i$ is the number of actions of player~$i$. Since this number grows exponentially with the number of players, these guarantees suffer from the curse of multiagency. We design an algorithm whose sample complexity depends on the action sets only through the largest individual action-set size $A_*:=\max_iA_i$. The algorithm uses $\widetilde O(SA_*H^5D^4/\varepsilon^4)$ transition samples and, with probability at least $1-δ$, returns a Markov policy that is an $\varepsilon$-robust CCE from every initial state. Here $S$ is the number of states, $H$ is the horizon, and $D:=\max_i\min\{H,1/ρ_i\}$, where $ρ_i$ is the TV radius of player~$i$. Two ingredients make this possible: a plug-in estimate of the robust backup whose mean error does not depend on the number of states, and a single batch of samples per round that serves as bandit feedback for all players at once. The result shows that the dependence on $\prod_iA_i$ can be avoided without modifying the $(s,a)$-rectangular uncertainty set.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Are We Rewarding the Wrong Module? Module-Specific Policy Optimization for Multimodal Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Artificial Intelligence 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AT-RL, PEARL, VQA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has been widely adopted to enhance the multimodal reasoning of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing methods share a common limitation of Module-Level Credit Entanglement, in which the vision encoder, projector, and LLM modules are jointly optimized using the same advantage signals even when perceptual correctness and final-answer correctness differ. To address this limitation, we propose Module-Specific Policy Optimization (MSPO), a framework that separates perceptual and outcome-based learning signals across model modules. Specifically, MSPO uses responses to perception checklists comprising auxiliary questions about visual information to derive an advantage for training the vision encoder. From these responses, MSPO computes the Confidence-aware Perception Reward (CaPR), using a perception sign obtained by thresholding checklist accuracy to determine reward direction and posterior confidence from a Beta distribution over latent perception accuracy to scale reward magnitude. We then derive the vision advantage through group-relative normalization of CaPR and scale it using main-task success rates. This advantage guides updates to the vision encoder, while the projector and LLM retain outcome-based GRPO updates. Extensive experiments on seven mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that MSPO outperforms existing RL-tuned models on average at both the 3B and 7B scales.

</details>

《TeamFlowRL: Online Policy Optimization of Decentralized Flow Policies for Multi-Agent Coordination》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MARL, TeamFlowRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Complex cooperative tasks require coordinated multi-agent behavior. Offline generative policies learn expressive cooperative behaviors from trajectories but remain limited by fixed datasets. Online reinforcement learning can improve them through environment interaction. However, fine-tuning flow policies is difficult because action likelihoods are hard to compute. We propose TeamFlowRL, a two-stage framework that pretrains multi-agent flow policies offline and fine-tunes them online. We formulate team policy optimization over the complete denoising trajectories of decentralized flow policies, avoiding intractable action likelihoods. The resulting objective weights each agent's trajectory ratio with a shared team advantage and clips each ratio separately, aligning local policy updates with the common team return. Across 51 paired configurations in seven simulation groups, TeamFlowRL improves every offline initialization, with a 47.3% average gain in normalized score. It also achieves 33.1% higher average success under exploration noise than Gaussian-policy fine-tuning across 6 bimanual tasks.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Assessing Robustness to Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Deep Reinforcement Learning for Cyber-Physical Systems》归入 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

As the use of reinforcement learning (RL) becomes more widespread in cyber-physical systems, it becomes increasingly important to design RL agents that are able to adapt to changes in their environments in order to maintain safety and reliability. This paper addresses the question of whether different classes of RL algorithm are more robust against forgetting previously learned behaviours, a critical issue in continual learning, in non-stationary environments. We evaluate the robustness of representative examples of three important classes of RL algorithm in a non-stationary autonomous parking environment. These are Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), which is a model-free on-policy algorithm, Soft-Actor-Critic (SAC), which is a model-free off-policy algorithm, and Drama, which is a model-based algorithm. The agents must learn to successfully park in four different scenarios, which are presented successively, with parking spaces oriented at varying angles. We find off-policy algorithms appear to be more robust compared to on-policy algorithms due to their use of replay, while model-based algorithms do not appear to offer any direct advantage over model-free algorithms. These experiments also highlight the additional challenges of finding robust abstractions of the environment, and oversensitivity to hyperparameters.

</details>

- **作者**: Jianhong Pang, Ziyi Ye, Qiuxia Hou, Yanhao Zhang, Zuxuan Wu

#### 研究背景与动机

《More Powerful, Less Verifiable? Mitigating Hard-to-Verify Hallucinations in MLLM's Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning (RL) strengthens model capabilities through feedback from humans and environments. However, emerging evidence suggests that reward optimization can produce outputs whose errors are harder for humans to detect. To address this issue, we construct ITHV (the Image-Text Hallucination Verifiability Dataset), comprising 2,728 visually grounded instances that distinguish between obvious hallucinations, which humans readily detect, and elusive hallucinations, which humans struggle to identify. We then propose DAPO-V, an extension of DAPO that maximizes correctness subject to a budget on verifiability-aware risk. Specifically, DAPO-V combines a verifiability-aware reward with adaptive dual control, where larger penalties are assigned to incorrect responses on questions with lower human verification accuracy. An adaptive multiplier further adjusts the penalty strength according to risk-budget violations. Across three MLLM base models, DAPO-V improves overall accuracy while substantially improving accuracy on elusive hallucination questions by 5.78--9.72 percentage points over accuracy-only DAPO. We further test DAPO-V with a blinded human study, which shows that hallucinations are more readily detected in DAPO-V than those of the base model.

</details>

- **作者**: Chenhao Wang, Haonan Ma, Wenjie Zhang, Yifan Wang, Mingbin Gao, Yuan-Ye Zhang, Wenjing Hong

#### 研究背景与动机

《MIFLOW: Dynamic Recent-Behavior Priors for Reference-Calibrated Online Flow-Policy Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：FlowRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Flow-based policies provide expressive action distributions for continuous-control reinforcement learning. In online actor-critic learning, off-policy value estimates can be unreliable in poorly covered action regions, where expressive policies may shift probability mass toward critic-favored actions. We propose MIFLOW, which calibrates online flow-policy learning with a recency-adaptive flow-matching prior fitted to recently executed actions. Inspired by canalization as a qualitative analogy for path-dependent stabilization, we use the prior as a moving density reference for value-guided policy improvement. MIFLOW uses the approximate recent-action log-density in a dimension-normalized correction to both actor updates and critic targets. This correction increases the cost of low-density actions while keeping them available. Across six MuJoCo continuous-control tasks and five random seeds, MIFLOW achieves competitive mean return curves and higher late-training mean returns than the evaluated baselines on several tasks. In a Walker2d-v4 case study, MIFLOW shows a lower proportion of actions assigned low density by the learned reference, both overall and among high-Q actions. Prior-family and prior-buffer comparisons show task-dependent differences in returns and prior-defined diagnostics. These results support dynamic recent-behavior priors for reference calibration in the evaluated settings. The diagnostics measure how sampled actions align with the learned reference. True action support and critic extrapolation error remain unmeasured in these experiments.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning enhanced Federated Learning for Predicting Remaining Useful Lifetime》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Remaining useful life (RUL) prediction is central to Prognostics and Health Management (PHM). The growth of industrial IoT has unlocked large multivariate sensor streams and enabled deep models - especially Transformers - for long-horizon sequence learning. However, transformers are parameter-heavy and data-hungry, while operational datasets are often siloed and privacy-sensitive. Federated Learning (FL) addresses this by training across clients without raw data sharing, but in practice many clients connect over lossy wireless links where UDP is preferred for low latency, making model exchange vulnerable to packet drops. We present a cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) framework that adapts FL to lossy channels by selecting per-client layer subsets to transmit and capping retransmissions. This framework is trained with QMIX value-function factorization for scalability. We instantiate the approach with a Graph Convolution Position-Aware Transformer (GCPATr) for RUL forecasting. On the CMAPSS dataset, our MARL-enhanced FL (RL-FL) consistently improves mean square error over standard FL, with the largest gains at high packet loss (e.g., up to 18% improvement on FD003 at 50% loss). These results show that MARL is an effective countermeasure to wireless degradation in FL-based RUL prediction.

</details>

- **作者**: Jeong Woon Lee, Namho Kim, Hyoseok Hwang
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 机器人, 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《POISE: Curvature-Aware Critic Regularization for Smooth Actor-Critic Control》归入 强化学习、机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：LipsNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Actor-critic methods achieve high returns in robotic control, yet trained actors exhibit high-frequency action oscillations. Most smoothness methods constrain the actor, whereas PAVE regularizes the critic by separately penalizing action-gradient variation and encouraging negative action curvature. The same gradient change, however, moves the critic's preferred action farther where the critic is flat, and separate penalties do not measure this motion. We characterize this motion through the greedy shift, which approximates greedy-action displacement across state transitions to first order under local concavity. Since displacement alone does not reflect its critic-value consequences, the analysis further shows that the shift's curvature-weighted energy approximates the value lost by retaining the previous greedy action to second order. Based on this relationship, POISE regularizes the curvature-weighted energy along temporal and spatial state displacements. It employs a saddle-free curvature metric and reward-scale-consistent scaling while leaving the actor unchanged. On six continuous control tasks with TD3, POISE reduces the smoothness score on every task by $5.9\times$ on average ($4.5\times$ geometric mean), compared with $2.2\times$ ($1.9\times$) for the strongest baseline, and achieves the lowest score on five tasks. Paired return intervals include zero on four tasks, with a 14% increase on Ant and an 11% decrease on Hopper.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Curvature-Equilibrated_Policy_Optimization_for_Deep_Reinforcement_Learning|Curvature-Equilibrated Policy Optimization for Deep Reinforcement Learning]]

![[assets/2611.31680_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.31680](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31680)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.31680

- **作者**: Gang Chen
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 世界模型
- **相关性评分**: 2.92（加权：强化学习 2.76，世界模型 0.16）

#### 研究背景与动机

《Curvature-Equilibrated Policy Optimization for Deep Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Gradient-based actor optimization is central to deep reinforcement learning. However, conventional first-order optimizers do not explicitly exploit the local curvature of neural actor losses. We propose EQUIP (Curvature-Equilibrated Gradients for Policy Search), a novel approach that seeks to balance local contraction and expansion of the actor parameter space by equilibrating positive and negative curvature. EQUIP constructs a trajectory-informed search subspace and uses the projected Hessian of actor loss to build a bounded local metric for curvature equilibration. First-order loss matching preserves the approximated first-order decrease in actor loss. Meanwhile, gradient mixing and curvature-based gradient selection adjust the gradient applied for actor optimization. We establish the geometric properties of curvature equilibration, derive bounds on gradient alignment and magnitude, and identify conditions for improved actor loss reduction and convergence toward stationarity under direct gradient updates. We integrate EQUIP into Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) without modifying its actor loss, critic learning, or replay mechanism. Experiments on 6 MuJoCo continuous control benchmarks show that the resulting EQUIP-SAC algorithm achieves higher mean sample efficiency than SAC and two second-order policy optimization baselines. Ablation studies further highlight the benefits of curvature equilibration and trajectory-informed geometry for policy training.

</details>
