实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EconGym。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Can artificial intelligence reproduce the economic dynamics that emerge as households, firms and governments interact and adapt through markets and institutions? To answer this question, we define an economic world model (EWM) as an agent's internal representation of how an economic system works, enabling counterfactual simulation and decision-making under resource constraints. We then propose a general framework for studying EWMs that links three tasks: simulating agent behaviour, learning economic transitions and systematically evaluating the dynamics. As an initial implementation, we learn household policies from microeconomic data and calibrate a structural economic transition model using macroeconomic data, linking individual decisions to aggregate dynamics. Our micro-to-macro evaluation framework compares simulated individual actions and group distributions with observed microdata and assesses macroeconomic dynamics through forecasting and counterfactual experiments. Results show that simulated and observed household consumption distributions overlap by 94.9%, while EWM forecasts closely track real GDP and several other aggregate levels. In government-borrowing counterfactuals, the EWM reproduces the benchmark life-cycle model's short-run transmission from borrowing to consumption. For ex ante planning, the EWM enables an LLM agent to achieve 10.29% higher simulated GDP under the epidemic shock than direct LLM decisions. This initial EWM is only a starting point. We invite economists and AI researchers to build a stronger EWM together. Economists can contribute richer economic models and knowledge, while AI researchers can develop stronger data-driven methods for simulating agent behaviour and learning economic transitions. Together, we aim not only to reproduce real economic dynamics, but also to discover and test new mechanisms that support better decisions and benefit society.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/Reliability-aware_AI_Agent_Execution_in_Collaborative_Edge_Computing_A_World_Model_Approach|Reliability-aware AI Agent Execution in Collaborative Edge Computing: A World Model Approach]]

![[assets/2611.32520_figure.jpg|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32520](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32520)

- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/Reliability-aware_AI_Agent_Execution_in_Collaborative_Edge_Computing_A_World_Model_Approach|Reliability-aware AI Agent Execution in Collaborative Edge Computing: A World Model Approach]]
- **作者**: Xiangchun Chen, Jiannong Cao, Longxiang Gao, Xiaoming Wu, Yusheng Ji
- **cs 子类**: cs.DC
- **归属领域**: 世界模型
- **相关领域**: 世界模型, 大模型

- **关联关键词**: Agent, WorldModel, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《Reliability-aware AI Agent Execution in Collaborative Edge Computing: A World Model Approach》归入 世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Collaborative edge computing supports AI agent applications by distributing execution across heterogeneous user-controlled devices with limited resources. This paper studies reliability-aware AI agent execution under node failures and wireless disruptions. These disruptions can make agent execution state unavailable or inconsistent, forcing recovery or recomputation that consumes the remaining deadline budget. Maintaining recoverable execution states can therefore improve request completion before deadlines. We formulate an online AI agent execution management problem that jointly selects resource allocations, state protection actions, and recovery targets to maximize the expected task success rate subject to resource, compatibility, and deadline constraints. We develop a world model that jointly captures evolving agent workflows and recoverable execution state under candidate management actions, accounting for node reliability, network conditions, and resource availability. The model enables the controller to simulate future execution and recovery trajectories under candidate actions and estimate their effects on execution progress and task completion before deadlines. We then employ world model-based model predictive control (WorldMPC) with Monte Carlo rollouts to jointly optimize resource allocation, state protection, and recovery decisions for long-term reliable agent execution under the dynamic environment. We evaluate the proposed approach on the simulation and real-world testbed using real-world and synthetic datasets. Experimental results show that WorldMPC improves task success rate over baselines in both simulation and real-world testbed.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/CriticJEPA_World_Models_Are_Better_Critics_Than_Optimizers|CriticJEPA: World Models Are Better Critics Than Optimizers]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《CriticJEPA: World Models Are Better Critics Than Optimizers》归入 世界模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Visual world models enable goal-directed planning by predicting action outcomes. Gradient-based planners optimize actions to lower predicted goal cost, but continued optimization can exploit prediction errors after execution stops improving. Comparing plans requires informative relative scores; improving them also requires reliable update directions. Exact-state execution reveals that a frozen hierarchical world model ranks goal-conditioned proposals even when its rankings among outputs of prolonged gradient search are uninformative. Effective planning pairs goal-directed candidates with predictions that distinguish their outcomes. We introduce CriticJEPA, which learns goal-conditioned flow proposals from reward-free trajectories and uses frozen rollouts to select among them at every hierarchy level. Forward selection forms a complete planner; short anchored updates refine candidates before selection. Across visual navigation and manipulation, CriticJEPA increases success at a fraction of the planning compute, transferring shared settings across independently trained models, hierarchy depths, and unseen tasks while keeping every world-model weight fixed.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《PhysRF-WorldJEPA: Transforming Heterogeneous Large Language Models into Manifold-Decoupled Millimeter-Wave Radar World Models》归入 世界模型、大模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、大模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：GQA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar enables privacy-preserving, all-weather physical scene understanding across darkness, smoke, and walls. However, standard instruction tuning treats continuous radar streams as static text-like tokens, causing near-random global radar-to-text retrieval and severe physical drift under sensor blackouts. Conversely, coupling pairwise contrastive losses (e.g., SigLIP) directly into the shared backbone improves retrieval but injects $O(B^2)$ repulsive gradients that fracture continuous coordinate manifolds ($R^2 &lt; 0$). We present PhysRF-WorldJEPA, a manifold-decoupled Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) that separates continuous electromagnetic state prediction from cross-modal language alignment. An unattenuated 1.0x clock anchor and parallel dual-scale RF encoder feed a lean ~3% LoRA LLM trained with two-step state-space EMA-JEPA (Denoise -&gt; Rollout) and sample-axis Batch-VICReg to predict +3.0 s physical states; a stop-gradient alignment head then maps frozen prompt states onto the text hypersphere with false-negative masking. On a 4,541-window tri-domain suite (77/60.25/60 GHz) against 10 baselines, freezing the physical backbone and training only the decoupled head lifts R-&gt;T R@10 from 5.95% to 15.01% and improves MedRank from 107.0 to 60.0 while preserving identical physical readout: 3.37 deg ($R^2=+0.961$) blackout +3.0 s torso tilt, 1.89 m ($R^2=+0.679$; 1.19 m/+0.863 at the RF front-end) cluttered UAV altitude, 2.04 m ($R^2=+0.461$) behind-wall NLOS range, 0.9976 virtual-RIS invariance, and 100% fall recall. Across five 0.5B-1.8B LLM families (15 zero-overhead models), World-JEPA improves retrieval and 75%-drop completion in 10/10 runs and blackout tilt in 4/5 (Span-Euc), 2/5 (Scatter), and 5/5 (per-family best) over SFT.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/ReJEPA_Bounded_Predictive_Regulation_for_Latent_World_Models|ReJEPA: Bounded Predictive Regulation for Latent World Models]]

![[assets/2611.31910_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.31910](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31910)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.31910
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/ReJEPA_Bounded_Predictive_Regulation_for_Latent_World_Models|ReJEPA: Bounded Predictive Regulation for Latent World Models]]
- **作者**: Fangzhou Lin, Yuping Wang, Shuo Xing, Yang Chen, Yuwen Heng, Xin Li, Wenbin He, Yuhan Yao, Zhengzhong Tu, Liu Ren
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 世界模型
- **相关领域**: 世界模型, 强化学习
- **相关性评分**: 1.32（加权：强化学习 0.16，世界模型 1.16）
- **关联关键词**: Agent, Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《ReJEPA: Bounded Predictive Regulation for Latent World Models》归入 世界模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕论文提出的建模、算法或系统设计进行实验验证。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Latent world models learn representations by predicting future states in an abstract embedding space rather than reconstructing raw pixels. When training across multi-frame sequences, however, not all frames and spatial regions are equally informative or predictable. We argue that forcing a world model to minimize every prediction error may be unnecessary and harmful. Under standard $L_2$ loss, unbounded gradients force the encoder and predictor to overfit noisy or transient frames at the expense of core physical structure, while amplifying gradient conflict with representation regularizers. Conversely, $L_1$ loss bounds large gradients, but its constant gradient magnitude near zero disrupts the local smoothness required for planning. We resolve this tension with \textbf{ReJEPA}, a bounded predictive regulation principle that combines bounded gradients for large errors with smooth quadratic behavior near zero, instantiated simply via the element-wise $\log\cosh$ loss. As a drop-in training replacement across Temporal Straightening, LeWorldModel, and V-JEPA~2.1, $\log\cosh$ consistently outperforms $L_2$, a curvature-matched quadratic control, $L_1$, and Smooth-$L_1$ baselines without altering architectures or inference procedures. Notably, the gains grow with environmental complexity, scaling from simple navigation to contact-rich manipulation and real-world autonomous driving on NAVSIM. Probing and geometric analyses confirm that relaxing the penalty on extreme errors yields representations that better capture physical states and align with the true planning cost landscape.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《CaLA: Causal Latent Adjustment for World Models under Adaptive Policies》归入 世界模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models is answering ``imagine" answers to ``what-if'' questions via imagined rollouts. But rollouts collected by adaptive policies induce treatment-outcome confounding in imagined rollouts. We study this problem in Match-3 difficulty adjustment, where latent player skill influences both assigned difficulty and churn. We build a fully specified Match-3 SCM with sequential mechanics, adaptive assignment, and paired ground-truth interventions, on which unadjusted models fit to the logged trajectories select difficulty settings that raise churn. We then introduce Causal Latent Adjustment (CaLA), a DAG-constrained world model that infers a variational player representation from pretreatment history. Across our evaluations, CaLA provides the most reliable population-level intervention estimates and competitive individualized decisions, although rich handcrafted histories can remain advantageous for individual-level adjustment. We further find that residual correction can degrade causal decision quality despite improving predictive fit, highlighting the need to distinguish predictive accuracy, confounding adjustment and the behavior of downstream causal estimators.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Exploratory Midtraining for Robot Policies》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot policies predominantly learn from human-collected data emphasizing competent behavior, limiting coverage of the low-level behaviors needed to support autonomous improvement. We study how a form of midtraining can be used to build coverage over diverse low-level behaviors that connect challenging states of the robot and its environment to states from which further progress is possible ("connected behavioral coverage"). We also examine how this coverage supports downstream adaptation and online improvement. To this end, we introduce $β$-Data, a dataset collected via adversarial teleoperation, where a human adversary reversibly perturbs the environment while a human operator continues toward completion without resetting. We relabel trajectory segments with local outcomes in natural language and finetune a pretrained policy conditioned on recent action history. The resulting policy exhibits sustained use of more low-level behaviors. Across manipulation tasks in simulation and on three real-robot platforms, our approach yields more favorable data scaling laws, better zero-shot generalization, and more effective use of additional rollout time than training on standard demonstrations. During online finetuning, these policies need fewer rollouts to collect successful experience and reach higher success rates, suggesting that midtraining can prepare robots for both more robust task execution and continued autonomous improvement. Our project page and robot videos are available at https://kshitiz-1225.github.io/exploratory-midtraining-robot-policies/.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/CAPER_PartGround_and_PlanRefine_for_Reliable_Manual-Guided_Robotic_Assembly_Planning|CAPER: PartGround and PlanRefine for Reliable Manual-Guided Robotic Assembly Planning]]

![[assets/2611.32986_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32986](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32986)

#### 研究背景与动机

《CAPER: PartGround and PlanRefine for Reliable Manual-Guided Robotic Assembly Planning》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：IsaacSim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language-Models (VLMs) used in robot task planning to derive assembly procedures from existing manuals may correctly interpret individual assembly actions yet confuse exact part identities, predict unsupported Part IDs, or produce inconsistent parts across assembly steps. Thus, we propose CAPER (Capacity-Aware Part-grounding and Evidence Refinement), a manual-guided robotic assembly framework that enables VLMs to generate assembly procedures with two modules--PartGround and PlanRefine. PartGround aligns step-level manual evidence with product-specific inventory and part-reference information to identify exact Part IDs and quantities, transforming free-form hardware prediction into evidence-guided assignments represented in a shared Assembly Intermediate Representation (Assembly IR). PlanRefine audits PartGround assignments against finite inventory by removing the lowest-confidence assignments. Uncertain identity cases remain marked for review in the Assembly IR. We evaluate CAPER on the IKEA Assembly in the Wild (IAW) dataset. Experiment results show that CAPER reaches a Finite-inventory Consistency Rate of 83.33% and produces zero observed out-of-inventory Part-ID occurrences. At the plan level, PlanRefine removes all observed inventory-overuse pairs (9 -&gt; 0) and excess units (51 -&gt; 0). Overall, the results indicate that PartGround and PlanRefine improve part reliability, while also enhancing finite-inventory consistency and local prediction accuracy. Project page and video: https://xhzzzz123.github.io/CAPER/

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Self-Sensing Compliant Anthropomorphic Hand for Speed-Dictated Shared Force Control in Teleoperation》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

In vision-based teleoperation of robotic hands, the operator receives no haptic feedback and therefore struggles to regulate the grasp force, while adding tactile sensors to the fingers increases wiring, cost and integration complexity. Compliant hands offer an alternative, as their mechanical compliance, usually treated only as a passive property for safe and adaptive grasping, can also serve as a source of information. Here, we show that the elastic transmission of a compliant, tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand can itself act as the sensor for shared-control telemanipulation. Series springs in the tendon routing are read by potentiometers in a forearm-mounted motor box, from which the hand estimates its joint states and finger forces without any external sensors. Building on this self-sensing, we develop a contact-aware shared-control framework for VR-based teleoperation. The robot hand is controlled through a mapping from the operator's hand kinematics until a contact is detected proprioceptively. Control then switches to a finger-level force controller whose set point is determined by the operator's grasping speed, and control authority can be allocated to individual fingers. Experiments validate the system's force estimation and increased grasp success rate compared with direct teleoperation for fragile and heavy objects. These results show that compliance can serve not only for passive adaptation but also as an intrinsic sensing resource for physical human-robot collaboration.

</details>

《Risk-Informed Path Planning: Collision and Grounding Avoidance Application for Autonomous Surface Vessels》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Maritime Autonomous Surface Ships (MASS) must demonstrate safety before and during operations, necessitating online risk assessment to guide decision-making at sea. This paper presents a risk-informed Rapidly Exploring Random Tree Star (RRT*) path planner that integrates a Bayesian Belief Network (BBN) as an online risk model. The BBN evaluates the probability and severity distribution of hazardous events, which are aggregated into a bounded scalar risk index. During the search, this risk index is evaluated at sample points along candidate edges. The planner optimizes a composite cost function comprising path distance, heading change penalties, and accumulated risk, subject to a hard constraint enforcing a risk acceptance limit. Each search tree vertex stores the BBN posterior at its state, enabling the tree to function as an explainable risk map for individual hazards. We define design requirements for the BBN to serve as the risk model in this context and introduce a runtime model that translates replanning deadlines into strict limits on single risk calculation times. The planner is demonstrated in a simulated harbor entry scenario involving collision and grounding hazards. Measured run times validate that the planner operates within the computational bounds predicted by the runtime model for a single replanning cycle.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Seeing to Drive on a Single Board: Attention-Free Depth for Onboard Robot Navigation》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ImageNet, MobileNet, ResNet, U-Net。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A small ground robot can carry one camera and a few watts of compute, but the depth foundation models that could turn that camera into a range sensor are vision transformers sized for GPUs. We bring one to a Raspberry Pi 5 with the Hailo-8L, an accelerator that runs every layer in integer arithmetic, attention included. Quantised for it, Depth Anything V2 loses large parts of the scene, and we measure why: at 449 tokens, 91% of softmax weights fall below one int8 step while carrying 17-33% of the attention mass. Our answer is to distil, not quantise. An attention-free ResNet-34 U-Net student of Depth Anything V2-Large (24.5M parameters) runs at 27.1 FPS on the chip, its int8 output matches its float twin (Pearson 0.99993), and on held-out scenes of its simulated yard it is the most accurate depth network on the chip (AbsRel 0.269 against 0.354-0.530). On 1,000 stereo-verified points of a real outdoor drive it is not resolvably different from the vendor's Depth Anything V2-Small build, while running faster (27.1 vs 21.6 FPS). Ranging obstacles by where they meet the floor needs only the camera height, so mapping, hybrid-A* planning and SLAM run on the Pi's own CPU. In real-time hardware-in-the-loop tests in a Gaussian-splat reconstruction of a real yard, the stack reaches the goal without contact in 7.9$\pm$1.1 of 10 scenes, against 9.2$\pm$0.4 with exact obstacles, and in 7.7$\pm$0.7 on its own pose with simulated wheel odometry (ten seeds each; 8.2$\pm$0.4 with the real Pi and Hailo-8L in the loop, five seeds). The source code, pre-trained models and data are available at https://github.com/itaim18/DANIEL.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《DeCoNav-Act: Benchmarking Collective Physical Capability in Multi-Robot Mobile Manipulation》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CoCoBench, EmbodiedBench, MAGMA-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot teams can split workloads or combine complementary bodies, but only if each robot leaves the shared world in a state its teammate can use. Existing multi-robot benchmarks emphasize task allocation and skill sequencing, rarely testing whether the resulting physical states are usable. To evaluate collective physical capability, we introduce DeCoNav-Act, a benchmark for language-guided multi-robot mobile manipulation in a shared physics simulation. Its 350 homogeneous and 150 heterogeneous tasks, each verified by joint reference execution, span two coordination mechanisms: parallel workloads in shared space and physical dependencies in which one robot establishes a condition for a teammate to use. Beyond team success, DeCoNav-Act measures path efficiency and handoff coverage and success, and adds a diagnostic from verified pre-handoff states. Evaluating sensor-grounded adaptations of ASPIRE and RAX and our agent CoMoMa, we find that with GPT-5.6-luna no method completes a task, at most 8.8% of dependencies are reached, and even from pre-handoff states no handoff is completed; with GPT-6.1-sol, CoMoMa establishes the condition in 26.5% of trials, but only 4.1% succeed.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/ICLWorld_Benchmarking_In-Context_Learning_of_Agents_from_Digital_to_Physical_Worlds|ICLWorld: Benchmarking In-Context Learning of Agents from Digital to Physical Worlds]]

![[assets/2611.32649_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32649](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32649)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32649
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/ICLWorld_Benchmarking_In-Context_Learning_of_Agents_from_Digital_to_Physical_Worlds|ICLWorld: Benchmarking In-Context Learning of Agents from Digital to Physical Worlds]]
- **作者**: Qingle Liu, Jieming Li, Pei-lin Li, Shenghan Zheng, Ruize Yang, Sunqi Fan, Deyao Hong, Huan-ang Gao, Meng-Hao Guo, Shuojin Yang
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 大模型, 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《ICLWorld: Benchmarking In-Context Learning of Agents from Digital to Physical Worlds》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AndroidWorld, CLBench, ICLWorld, MMCL-Bench, OSWorkerBench, OSWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

In-context learning (ICL) lets agents acquire new rules from context and adapt to user-specific requirements at inference time. The same frontier agents now operate both computers and robots, yet their ICL remains insufficiently assessed: existing benchmarks are largely offline, rely on text or static images, and risk training-data contamination, while robot evaluations are limited to method-level ablations. We present ICLWorld, an online benchmark that evaluates the ICL of agents from digital to physical worlds, covering computer use and robot use, together with UIverse, a unified harness that turns every environment into a user interface. We build 30 environments, record 630 demonstration videos (10+ hours), and design 160 tasks with customized, often counter-intuitive rules that cannot be satisfied without learning from the context. Each task comes with three rule versions, given as text or video, and we test generalization at three levels, from replaying the demonstration to new instances and unseen environments. Across six frontier models, the strongest agents, Claude Opus 5.5 and GPT-6 Astra, succeed on 82% and 76% of computer-use runs but only 27% and 26% of robot-use runs, and a no-rule control reduces success to at most 8%. GPT-6 Astra succeeds under all three rule versions on only 38% of computer-use tasks, half its rate under a single rule, and answers 98% of robot rule questions correctly yet completes only 20% of these tasks. Frontier agents learn rules comparably well from video and from text and transfer them across environments, whereas weaker agents rely on text and lose most of their video-based success once the demonstrated situation is resampled. These findings reveal substantial gaps between the digital and the physical world and between identifying a rule and acting on it.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《An XR-Based System for Enhanced Spatial Awareness in Mobile Robot Teleoperation》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Teleoperation of mobile robots represents a critical capability for operations in hazardous environments, yet maintaining adequate situational awareness remains challenging without effective visualization and control interfaces. This work presents a multi-modal extended reality (XR) system for robot teleoperation and scene exploration that integrates immersive visual and haptic feedback modalities. To address motion sickness commonly induced by 360-degree camera feeds in head-mounted displays (HMDs), the system employs a fixed dual-camera configuration: a forward-facing camera for primary navigation and a rear-facing camera for enhanced situational awareness without operator discomfort. For navigation in confined or low-visibility conditions, a 16-channel Velodyne LiDAR generates real-time 2D occupancy grid maps, which are converted into vibrational haptic feedback on handheld controllers to provide intuitive, non-visual obstacle detection. User evaluation with 20 participants demonstrates that this haptic modality significantly reduces the cognitive load associated with obstacle avoidance during teleoperation.

</details>

![[assets/2611.32579_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32579](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32579)

- **作者**: Hanjing Ye, Tianle Zeng, Jianwei Peng, Yanci Wen, Yuchen Zhou, Yonggen Ling, Hong Zhang
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Follow-Bench 2.0: An End-to-End 3D Benchmark for Socially-Aware Robot Person Following》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EVT-Bench, Follow-Bench, TPT-Bench, TrackVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Socially-aware robot person following (RPF) requires a mobile robot to follow a designated person in dynamic human environments while maintaining target identity, avoiding obstacles and pedestrians, and preserving socially comfortable formations. Existing benchmarks only partially evaluate this coupled problem: embodied visual tracking benchmarks emphasize target visibility with limited social interaction, person re-identification benchmarks study perception without closed-loop control, and motion-planning benchmarks often assume ground-truth target and pedestrian states. We introduce Follow-Bench 2.0, an end-to-end 3D benchmark for evaluating socially-aware RPF under coupled perception--planning challenges. Built in Unreal Engine, Follow-Bench 2.0 provides difficulty-leveled scenarios with diverse pedestrian flows, lighting and weather conditions, cluttered layouts, bottlenecks, queueing behaviors, distractors, and temporary target occlusions. The benchmark supports modular perception--planning pipelines, end-to-end visual policies, and foundation-model-based active trackers under a unified closed-loop protocol. It further evaluates both back- and side-following configurations and reports safety--comfort metrics covering task success, target visibility, target recovery, social-space intrusion, following formation, and motion smoothness. Experiments show that RPF-oriented modular pipelines substantially outperform current end-to-end visual policies on close and socially comfortable following, but also reveal that socially-aware RPF remains far from solved in interaction-heavy environments and especially in side-following. Follow-Bench 2.0 therefore provides a diagnostic platform for studying how perception errors, occlusions, crowd interactions, and following configurations jointly affect robot person following.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Maps are widely used in the robotics community for localization and path planning and the ability to identify incorrect or inaccurate regions of a map is an area of active research interest. For large environments, manual edits of the map can be very time consuming and a tool which can assist a map editor is needed. With this paper, we contribute to robot introspection through self-assessment of map quality, using only a given map as input to be independent of the underlying mapping algorithm. Current methods for map quality assessment rely on ground-truth or labelled information for training classifiers, both of which can be difficult to obtain. In this paper, we propose a self-supervised learning approach to map quality assessment of 2D occupancy grid maps which identifies regions of a map that require further investigation. We leverage an autoencoder for patches extracted from the map to capture the dominant structure of the map. By evaluating the reconstruction loss of a patch reconstructed with the learned model, we can evaluate whether it contains an unusual structure, which indicates the need of further investigation. We evaluate our method on the outcome of state-of-the-art grid mapping methods using benchmark datasets as well as maps from warehouse environments, highlighting the application to more complex environments. We show that the method identifies regions with sparse data, clutter, and misregistrations.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《AirLeg: A Robust and Accurate Self-Supervised Visual-Inertial-Leg SLAM System》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Visual-inertial-leg (VIL) state estimation offers cost-effective localization for legged robots, adding only a camera to the onboard IMU and joint encoders. However, learning-based leg odometry is limited by its supervision: networks trained in simulation suffer from the sim-to-real gap, while real-world labels require costly sensors or inherit the errors of visual estimates. To address this, we present AirLeg, a self-supervised VIL SLAM system in which vision and leg proprioception improve each other from training to deployment. We pre-train the leg odometry network in simulation and adapt it to the robot without ground truth through imperative learning, where multi-sensor bundle adjustment uses the leg odometry to refine the visual map, which in turn supervises the network. During deployment, the fused estimates adapt the network online. To improve both localization and this supervision, a dual-window estimator fuses vision, IMU, and leg odometry, relates loaded feet to the visually reconstructed terrain through a deformation-aware constraint, and weights the constraints by variance component estimation. AirLeg completes all 17 GrandTour sequences with the lowest average error (0.19 m) among 19 methods. On a low-cost quadruped, it achieves the lowest drift (0.6%) with only 0.5 h of unlabeled data, in real time on an embedded computer.

</details>

《MRCT: An Object-Centric Two-Layer Control Architecture for Heavy-Payload Multi-Robot Transportation》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Cooperative transportation of heavy payloads by heterogeneous mobile manipulators requires coordinated payload motion, force distribution, and compliant physical interaction. This paper presents MRCT, an object-centric two-layer control architecture for multi-robot transportation. At the object level, a model predictive controller (MPC) generates a coordinated contact-force reference for the payload. At the robot level, whole- body impedance control (WBIC) modules on individual robots realise the reference through a confidence-based blending of velocity-tracking and impedance torques. The blending mechanism emphasises tracking when the joint-velocity error is small and increases compliance when tracking degrades because of abrupt reference changes or physical disturbances. Simulation and hardware experiments with a heterogeneous team comprising a single-arm and a dual-arm mobile manipulator transporting a near-rated table payload demonstrate accurate object tracking, regulated contact forces, and stable payload orientation. Parameter sensitivity studies, obstacle-avoidance transportation, and step-load disturbance experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness of MRCT for reliable cooperative transportation under the evaluated conditions

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《CoFiNav: VLM-Guided Coarse-to-Fine Semantic Navigation for Open-Ended Last-Mile UAV Delivery》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) delivery is an important application of the low-altitude economy, enhancing the flexibility and accessibility of last-mile logistics. Existing UAV delivery methods generally rely on precise terminal coordinates, predefined terminal specifications, or task-specific localization procedures, limiting the scope and flexibility of delivery. As UAV logistics becomes more intelligent, open-ended semantic descriptions offer a new paradigm for specifying terminal destinations. However, conventional aerial navigation methods have limited ability to generalize to delivery scenarios that require open-vocabulary terminal navigation. To enable this broader delivery paradigm, we propose CoFiNav, a VLM-guided coarse-to-fine UAV semantic navigation framework that progressively grounds relation-defined terminal destinations through hierarchical semantic search and active 3D observation. Given a task description, a coarse prior map from OpenStreetMap (OSM), and accumulated observations, CoFiNav uses a vision-language model (VLM) to determine which unresolved entity or spatial relation should be verified next. A geometry-aware module converts the semantic observation request into feasible viewpoints using RGB images and LiDAR, while conventional motion planning handles safe execution. Experiments across multiple urban environments show that CoFiNav outperforms representative adapted baselines in success rate while requiring shorter flight paths and fewer observations.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Facial robots require many coordinated, smallamplitude motions, but accommodating these motions in a compact face remains difficult. This paper presents an anatomically inspired facial-robot platform that reproduces the layered organization of the human facial musculoskeletal system. The platform comprises bone, muscle, and skin components whose geometry, shape, and attachment locations are derived from three-dimensional anatomical data. Wire-driven, compliant muscle components are routed through PTFE tubes, allowing actuation to be placed away from the face. We describe the selection and fabrication of 65 muscles (34 muscle types) required to reproduce the 47 target action units (AUs) in the Facial Action Coding System (FACS). We then evaluate the reproducibility of individual AUs in two stages: muscle actuation without skin and actuation after the silicone skin is attached. Forty of the 47 target AUs were reproduced with the muscle components, and 30 were reproduced after skin attachment. These results demonstrate the feasibility of reproducing a broad range of FACS facial expressions using an anatomically inspired musculoskeletal structure. The proposed platform provides a foundation for the systematic development and evaluation of future anatomically inspired facial robots

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Any2Act_Scaling_In-Context_Robot_Learning_from_Heterogeneous_Demonstrations|Any2Act: Scaling In-Context Robot Learning from Heterogeneous Demonstrations]]

![[assets/2611.32353_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32353](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32353)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32353
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Any2Act_Scaling_In-Context_Robot_Learning_from_Heterogeneous_Demonstrations|Any2Act: Scaling In-Context Robot Learning from Heterogeneous Demonstrations]]
- **作者**: Ziang Ren, Zifan Wang, Zhuoyang Li, Benhao Qin, Haoyang Li, Qingtao Liu, Xuchuan Chen, Zekun Qi, He Wang, Yanchao Yang, Li Yi
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.5（加权：具身智能 0.3，大模型 0.1，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Any2Act: Scaling In-Context Robot Learning from Heterogeneous Demonstrations》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot in-context learning (ICL) enables a policy to perform unseen tasks by following contextual demonstrations, without task-specific fine-tuning. This is increasingly valuable as large language models reasoning at low frequency can specify tasks through demonstrations and delegate high-frequency execution to such a policy. However, methods conditioned on human videos are bounded by scarce human-robot pairs, while those conditioned on robot demonstrations accept only one or two source types at limited scale. We present Any2Act, a foundation model for robot ICL. Our key insight is that a demonstration should be expressed in the camera layout and action space of the robot that performs it (the executor), not of whoever provided it (the demonstrator). This unified embodiment-grounded representation decouples learning to follow from admitting a new source. Any2Act learns to follow once, from robot-to-robot pairs that already take this form in 1.2M real trajectories spanning 10K tasks. Human videos, simulation, UMI, and other robots are then translated into this representation, and source-routed adapters trained on only a few hundred pairs each absorb the residual gap while the pretrained model stays frozen. For long horizons, a training-free tracker feeds only the demonstration window around the executor's progress, bounding the context and rolling back to redo failed steps. From a single demonstration, Any2Act achieves 57.5% success on unseen tasks without fine-tuning, exceeding VLAs fine-tuned on 50 demonstrations per task; other demonstrators retain 74-91% of the success achieved with the executor's own demonstrations.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/GOLD-SLAM_Gaussian-splatting_Omnidirectional_LiDAR-Depth_SLAM|GOLD-SLAM: Gaussian-splatting Omnidirectional LiDAR-Depth SLAM]]

![[assets/2611.32335_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Weicong Zhang, Yuan Huang, Yu Zheng, Lin Chenxiao, Yingying Li, Lilong Fan, Liangdong Zhuang, Yufan Chen, Ruiping Liu, Junwei Zheng, Jiaming Zhang

#### 研究背景与动机

《GOLD-SLAM: Gaussian-splatting Omnidirectional LiDAR-Depth SLAM》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Panoramic cameras capture nearly the entire spherical field of view in a single frame. However, the mismatch between this panoramic coverage and the field of view of LiDAR poses a substantial challenge for panoramic Gaussian splatting SLAM that relies on LiDAR depth priors. We introduce GOLD-SLAM, a Gaussian-splatting Omnidirectional LiDAR-Depth SLAM system that combines high-quality rendering with accurate geometric reconstruction. It performs tracking and mapping in the spherical domain and uses tracked visual features in regions without LiDAR coverage to improve pose estimation. To address insufficient coverage of newly observed surfaces and floating artifacts during incremental mapping, we introduce Depth-Aware Map Extension and Pruning (DAEP). To enable broader evaluation, we collect a new dataset, WHOLE, using a UAV platform across Weak-geometry, High-dynamic, Omnidirectional, Low-light, and Easy scenarios. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks and WHOLE demonstrate competitive rendering quality and improved geometric accuracy relative to perspective systems. Compared with existing panoramic baselines, GOLD-SLAM improves mean PSNR by 5.56-9.10 dB and reduces mean Depth-L1 by 71.9-83.9%. To assess deployment feasibility on resource-constrained platforms such as UAVs, we evaluate an embedded configuration of GOLD-SLAM on a Jetson Orin NX (16 GB), demonstrating online operation at the cost of reconstruction quality.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We put a Thor robot on a browser voice call, with speech sent from Safari, answers played through the robot's loudspeaker and transcripts returned to the phone. We separate HTTPS signaling, relayed WebRTC media and speech generation into independently observable routes. The browser prefetches relay credentials and allows a 500 ms window after its first relay candidate. The backend starts speech and WebRTC-answer preparation concurrently within a 55 s deadline. A session slot and a non-blocking speaker lock coordinate calls with local voice access. A 2 s gate gives the controller an opportunity to suppress chat before playback. Fifteen of sixteen first dials reached ready-to-speak, comprising seven of eight Wi-Fi calls with a median of 5.57 s and eight of eight cellular calls with a median of 6.76 s. All twenty scored occupancy and recovery scenarios admitted a later call. During five 60 s outages, the backend released the line while the phone remained offline. Across 40 controlled replays, enabling the gate suppressed chat when the controller decided at 1 s. At 3 s, chat and controller feedback both played in sequence, with no overlapping output in any trace. Two additional boundary traces exposed a receipt naming a later feedback item and a new call's query returning its predecessor's feedback. These measurements cover application events on one platform and simulated controller decisions, with acoustic output and task correctness outside their scope.

</details>

![[assets/2611.32233_figure.png|800]]

- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32233

- **作者**: Yujie Zhao, Anthony L. Hu, Zimeng Huang, Zhijing Wu, Luobin Wang, Richard Li, Shrivas Sudharsan, Henrik I. Christensen, Jishen Zhao
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《Roboclock: A Unified Benchmark for Time-Aware Robotic Intelligence》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DynamicVLA, OpenVLA, RLBench, ReflexBench, ReflexVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Time is a fundamental dimension of robotic intelligence: a robot may fail despite physically correct actions if it acts outside a valid time window, responds too slowly to an event, or fails to meet required throughput. Yet existing robotic benchmarks primarily emphasize spatial correctness and logical task completion, largely overlooking the temporal constraints of real-world applications. We present RoboClock, a unified benchmark for time-aware robotic manipulation featuring 60 tasks across five application domains. Mirroring these failure modes, RoboClock structures tasks around three types of temporal constraints: Temporal Window, Event-Reactive, and Throughput. Furthermore, RoboClock incorporates a Latency-Aligned evaluation protocol that continuously advances environment time during model inference and control-interface delays, paired with time-sensitive metrics. Evaluating over 30 methods, we find that the best-performing approach is the trained VLA, yet it achieves only 8.3% temporal success even on Easy-level tasks, with persistent challenges in generalization and transfer. These findings reveal fundamental limitations in current robotic systems and highlight the need to jointly optimize physical competence, temporal reasoning, and execution latency.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《OVOK: A Recursive Self-Improving Agentic Framework for Video-Based Articulated Robot State Estimation》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CtRNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recovering articulated robot state from video supports metric spatial sensing for manipulation, scene reconstruction, and action planning. Yet the problem is ill-posed: different intrinsics, camera-to-robot poses, and joint angles can explain similar observations. Prior task-specific regressors typically assume known intrinsics, fit one robot through laborious data collection and robot-specific training, and still transfer poorly across scenes. Foundation models apply to any robot zero-shot, but their estimates have large errors. We present OVOK (One Video to Kinetics), a recursive self-improving agentic framework for this task. Under a human-authored protocol, an agentic initializer constructs robot-specific OVOK-4k; a proposer--executor--verifier loop then jointly evolves tools and executable workflows from feedback. Updated tools and workflows are accepted only when the verifier judges a positive improvement. The retained workflow is frozen for accurate inference. Experiments demonstrate that the evolved workflow achieves state-of-the-art results on DREAM-V and RoboKeyGen-V, delivering robust and accurate estimates.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/RoboProbe_Benchmarking_Physical_Plausibility_of_Robot_World_Models|RoboProbe: Benchmarking Physical Plausibility of Robot World Models]]

![[assets/2611.32152_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.32152](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32152)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.32152

- **作者**: Zhuoying Yang, Jiachen Ni, Yichen Guo, Xiaoshan Wu, Mengyu Wang
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 世界模型, 具身智能, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《RoboProbe: Benchmarking Physical Plausibility of Robot World Models》归入 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：KineBench, PAI-Bench, PDI-Bench, RBench, RP-Bench, World-in-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Evaluating the physical plausibility of generated videos is critical for reliable robotic planning with world models. These videos inform action proposal and assessment before real-world execution, so undetected physical violations can lead to erroneous assessments of action feasibility and safety. Yet current video generators often produce physically implausible interactions. Detecting such violations directly with vision-language models (VLMs) remains challenging as physical verification requires evidence-grounded spatiotemporal reasoning beyond high-level semantic understanding. We develop five law-specific metrics for evaluating physical plausibility by combining spatiotemporal evidence from segmentation, tracking, 3D geometry, or appearance comparisons with targeted VLM reasoning: Contact fidelity, Object persistence, Solidity, Robot deformation, and Object deformation. Building on this metric suite, we introduce RoboProbe, an agentic evaluation framework that coordinates metric execution and selectively refines evaluations through constrained actions. To evaluate the physical plausibility of robot world models, we further introduce RP-Bench, with 761 human-labeled generated videos and 130 real demonstrations of 239 manipulation tasks from 12 robot datasets. On RP-Bench, all five metrics achieve AUROCs above 0.85 and outperform the evaluated learned judges, prior evaluators, and a frames-only Gemini baseline. Applying these metrics to five video generators reveals distinct patterns of physical violations across models, in both violation types and frequencies.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《MARGIN: A Risk-Aware Agent for Robot Program Refinement》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：RobotWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot-programming agents improve executable control policies through repeated testing and repair, yet binary success saturates while execution risk persists across unseen scenes. We introduce MARGIN, a risk-aware agent that converts successful robot rollouts into actionable evidence for program refinement. Its near-miss margin measures the fraction of the execution budget remaining in the slowest successful development rollout. The agent uses this signal to prioritize fragile programs, guide targeted repairs, and select revisions that preserve development success while increasing execution headroom. An exact safe-racing rule accelerates success-based candidate evaluation. We evaluate MARGIN across LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-90, and Robosuite. In our 80-task LIBERO-Pro reproduction, 78 programs attain perfect development success while accounting for substantial failures on unseen seeds. The near-miss margin predicts held-out failures with an AUC of 0.774, and low-margin programs exhibit 2.97 times higher failure risk. Across two independent evaluation rounds spanning 37 unique LIBERO-Pro tasks, MARGIN reduces executed control steps by 10.7% and 15.0%, with reductions of 18.0-20.0% on LIBERO-90 and Robosuite. Safe racing reduces fixed-sequence candidate-evaluation episodes by 53.5% while retaining identical selections, and paired execution probes improve acceptance decisions by identifying hidden regressions. Together, these results establish near-miss evidence as an effective basis for risk-aware, budget-efficient robot program refinement.

</details>

![[assets/2611.31925_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.31925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31925)

- **作者**: Yanran Jiang, Cécile Paris, Dana Kulić, Leimin Tian
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 强化学习

- **关联关键词**: Robotics, EmbodiedAI, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning When a Robot Should Ask For Help: Adaptive Intervention Requests in Human-Robot Teaming》归入 机器人、具身智能、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robots operating in complex environments occasionally encounter situations where autonomous navigation fails. Requesting and receiving a timely intervention from their human supervisor can be critical for failure prevention or recovery. However, requesting help too frequently or at inappropriate times can increase operator workload and reduce team efficiency, while delayed requests may lead to prolonged downtime or unrecoverable failures. In addition to alert timing, the human, robot and task context also influences the intervention outcomes. This paper proposes an algorithm for learning to generate adaptive human intervention requests during human-robot teaming. A help-seeking policy is trained via offline reinforcement learning based on past intervention helpfulness and autonomous progress. This learned alert policy enables a robot to selectively request human help when the intervention outcome is likely to be beneficial. We evaluate the proposed policy in different task environments and user expertise levels in simulation, followed by evaluation in a physical user study. Our findings demonstrate that the learned policy improves exploration efficiency, reduces unnecessary alerts, and enhances novice user support without compromising expert performance in real-time human-robot teaming missions.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/CALVIN-MAX_When_Does_a_Mid-Execution_Change_Hurt_a_Robot_Policy|CALVIN-MAX: When Does a Mid-Execution Change Hurt a Robot Policy?]]

![[assets/2611.31868_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.31868](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31868)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.31868
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/CALVIN-MAX_When_Does_a_Mid-Execution_Change_Hurt_a_Robot_Policy|CALVIN-MAX: When Does a Mid-Execution Change Hurt a Robot Policy?]]
- **作者**: Sajjad Pakdamansavoji
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 1.4（加权：具身智能 0.3，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《CALVIN-MAX: When Does a Mid-Execution Change Hurt a Robot Policy?》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DynamicVLA, SwitchVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robots must often keep working after the world changes, as when a block moves or the camera shifts while the robot still acts on what it saw before. Recent work showed that one change during a task lowers success for every policy tested, but did not separate the effect of its timing from the cost of the changed world, or measure what it costs afterwards. We introduce CALVIN-MAX, a benchmark of 7,828 cases on CALVIN's five-instruction chains, each with one change to the objects, the camera, the robot, or the instruction. Each case compares three runs that are identical until the affected instruction begins: one without the change, one with it in place from that moment, and one in which it arrives while the robot is moving. The second run against the first gives the cost of the changed world, and the third against the second the effect of meeting it mid-motion. On 1,526 held-out cases per policy, with hypotheses fixed in advance, each of ten released policies loses 4.4 to 9.9 percentage points on the affected instruction over the eight core changes. Whether timing matters depends on the change: one that alters what the robot sees costs 5.4 points less mid-motion than from the start, whereas a change to the scene itself or to the robot shows no detectable effect. Rewinding the robot to the start of the instruction removes the benefit for every policy; the stricter test we fixed in advance, that the rewound run then matches the second run, passes for three. The cost also outlasts the affected instruction: the change stays in place, and a policy that still completes that instruction is 5.0 to 16.8 points less likely to complete the next one.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Sparks_of_Weight-Space_Programming_for_Robot_Foundation_Models|Sparks of Weight-Space Programming for Robot Foundation Models]]

![[assets/2611.31715_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.31715](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31715)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.31715
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Sparks_of_Weight-Space_Programming_for_Robot_Foundation_Models|Sparks of Weight-Space Programming for Robot Foundation Models]]
- **作者**: Kyle Coelho, Eugen Solowjow

- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Sparks of Weight-Space Programming for Robot Foundation Models》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Robotics 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

This paper demonstrates an approach for correcting failures of robot foundation models (RFMs) without additional data collection, demonstrations, or training. We apply weight-space programming (WSP), in which a language-model agent directly reads and writes a neural network's parameters, to a large, pre-trained vision-language-action policy. The idea is simple: many policy failures are not random but repeatedly land in the same recognizable stuck state, so if that state is visible in the model's internal activations, a small edit could trigger a corrective movement while leaving the rest of the model's parameters unchanged. A core design choice is where to write the edit: repurposing a few near-silent neurons in the action expert lets the edit switch on in situations associated with the failure and produce a corrective movement, such as lowering the gripper toward the target, that is specified directly rather than learned through training. Empirically, when applied to the pi0.5 policy in simulation, we find that edits to roughly 0.0002% of the parameters can improve success on recurring failures, including for a policy that was already fine-tuned on its task. Reversed and random edits indicate that the gains come from the intended behavior, and the approach works best when the failure situation is recognizable and a simple correction can address it.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/CATWAY_Cost-sensitive_agentic_three-way_robot_decisions_with_calibrated_language-model_confidence|CATWAY: Cost-sensitive agentic three-way robot decisions with calibrated language-model confidence]]

![[assets/2611.31694_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.31694](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31694)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.31694
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/CATWAY_Cost-sensitive_agentic_three-way_robot_decisions_with_calibrated_language-model_confidence|CATWAY: Cost-sensitive agentic three-way robot decisions with calibrated language-model confidence]]
- **作者**: Erik Derner
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.65（加权：具身智能 0.3，大模型 0.25，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Agent, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《CATWAY: Cost-sensitive agentic three-way robot decisions with calibrated language-model confidence》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：SafeAgentBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A language-model robot agent must decide whether to execute, question or refuse an instruction, and an unsafe execution costs far more than an unneeded question. No prior robot gate derives all its thresholds from error costs or acts on calibrated probabilities, and none was fitted in one domain and evaluated unchanged in another. We propose CATWAY, a cost-sensitive three-way decision gate: a loss matrix over four instruction conditions (clear, ambiguous, infeasible, unsafe) fixes execute, ask and refuse regions on the probability simplex, the question being the information-gathering step. Its probabilities fuse a ten-sample vote with graded scores of ambiguity, infeasibility and risk by logistic regression. Fitted on household instructions and frozen, CATWAY was tested on text-level decisions of three small models in-domain, in two new robot domains and on the external TRUST-NAV benchmark. At eleven generations per decision against one, it was significantly cheaper than a direct prompt, a cost-aware prompt and an unguarded agent in all 18 household comparisons (mean loss per item 0.28 against 0.57-0.83), in 9 of 18 in the new domains and in 10 of 18 on TRUST-NAV (Holm per set), and never significantly costlier. It executed none of the 142 unsafe test instructions. On a unanimous vote, the minimum-expected-loss rule reduces calibration to one loss-derived threshold, so for two of six model configurations a gate on the vote alone never executed a clear instruction, costing 0.49 per item on TRUST-NAV against CATWAY's 0.13 and 0.09; the fused estimate avoided this on every set.

</details>

![[assets/2611.31341_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Xiao Wang, Lu Dong, Ifeoma Nwogu, Srirangaraj Setlur, Venu Govindaraju
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《EvoMisty: Building, Optimizing, and Evolving Capabilities on a Social Robot》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Social robots face diverse and changing tasks in companionship and education. LLM-based methods can generate task programs, but they still rely on manually developed control interfaces and predefined capabilities. They also lack a general mechanism to optimize completed tasks based on user-specified metrics and reuse performance improvements across tasks.We present EvoMisty, a plugin-based framework that enables coding agents to build and continually improve robot capabilities. EvoMisty builds a control program from official documentation and verifies candidate capabilities on a physical robot. Verified capabilities are stored in an atomic capability library and combined with existing skills to solve tasks. After task completion, EvoMisty can collect execution data, measure user-specified metrics, and iteratively improve task programs. It then distills reusable skills from final solutions into a skill library for future tasks.We evaluate EvoMisty on a physical Misty II robot using Claude Code and Codex. The two agents construct 109 and 107 verified atomic capabilities, respectively. Both complete all 28 AutoMisty benchmark tasks and seven additional tasks beyond AutoMisty's capabilities. Optimization improves all seven evaluated metrics across five tasks. For example, dialogue response latency decreases by 52% and 71%, respectively. Reusing skills from one optimized dialogue task reduces latency by 33% to 72% in three other dialogue tasks without further optimization. All code and experimental videos will be publicly released through the webpage: https://wangxiaoshawn.github.io/projects/evomisty/index.html

</details>
